import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Clock, Eye, User } from 'lucide-react';
import type { BlogPost } from '../../types';
import { formatDate } from '../../utils';
import { getUserById } from '../../data/users';

interface ArticleCardProps {
  post: BlogPost;
  href?: string;
  featured?: boolean;
}

export function ArticleCard({ post, href, featured = false }: ArticleCardProps) {
  const author = getUserById(post.authorId);

  const content = (
    <div
      className="card card-hover"
      style={{
        padding: 20,
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        gap: 12,
        height: '100%',
      }}
    >
      {/* Category + Reading time */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: 11,
            fontWeight: 600,
            color: 'var(--color-accent)',
            background: 'var(--color-accent-light)',
            padding: '3px 10px',
            borderRadius: 99,
          }}
        >
          {post.category}
        </span>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', fontSize: 12 }}>
          <Clock size={12} />
          {post.readingTime} min read
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Title */}
      <h3
        style={{
          fontSize: featured ? 17 : 15,
          fontWeight: 600,
          color: 'var(--color-text)',
          lineHeight: 1.4,
          flex: featured ? 0 : 1,
        }}
      >
        {post.title}
      </h3>

      {/* Excerpt */}
      <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.55, flex: 1 }}>
        {post.excerpt.length > 130 ? post.excerpt.slice(0, 130) + '…' : post.excerpt}
      </p>

      {/* Tags */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        {post.tags.slice(0, 3).map((tag) => (
          <span
            key={tag}
            style={{
              fontSize: 11,
              color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
              background: 'var(--color-bg)',
              border: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
              borderRadius: 4,
              padding: '2px 7px',
            }}
          >
            #{tag}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Footer */}
      <div
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          paddingTop: 10,
          borderTop: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
        }}
      >
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
          <User size={12} />
          {author?.name ?? 'Unknown'}
          <span>·</span>
          {formatDate(post.publishedAt)}
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
          <Eye size={12} />
          {post.views.toLocaleString()}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  if (href) {
    return (
      <Link to={href} style={{ display: 'block', height: '100%', textDecoration: 'none' }}>
        {content}
      </Link>
    );
  }

  return content;
}

FILE: src/components/ui/Button.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { cn } from '../../utils';

interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'accent' | 'ghost' | 'danger';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  leftIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  rightIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  loading?: boolean;
}

export function PrimaryButton({
  children,
  variant = 'primary',
  size = 'md',
  leftIcon,
  rightIcon,
  loading,
  className,
  disabled,
  ...props
}: ButtonProps) {
  const variantClass = {
    primary: 'btn-primary',
    secondary: 'btn-secondary',
    accent: 'btn-accent',
    ghost: 'btn-ghost',
    danger: 'btn-danger',
  }[variant];

  const sizeClass = {
    sm: 'btn-sm',
    md: '',
    lg: 'btn-lg',
  }[size];

  return (
    <button
      className={cn('btn', variantClass, sizeClass, className)}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      {...props}
    >
      {loading ? (
        <span
          style={{
            width: 14,
            height: 14,
            border: '2px solid currentColor',
            borderTopColor: 'transparent',
            borderRadius: '50%',
            animation: 'spin 0.7s linear infinite',
            display: 'inline-block',
            flexShrink: 0,
          }}
        />
      ) : (
        leftIcon && <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>{leftIcon}</span>
      )}
      {children}
      {rightIcon && !loading && (
        <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>{rightIcon}</span>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}

export function SecondaryButton(props: ButtonProps) {
  return <PrimaryButton variant="secondary" {...props} />;
}

export function GhostButton(props: ButtonProps) {
  return <PrimaryButton variant="ghost" {...props} />;
}

import React from 'react';

interface EmptyStateProps {
  icon?: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  action?: React.ReactNode;
}

export function EmptyState({ icon, title, description, action }: EmptyStateProps) {
  return (
    <div className="empty-state">
      {icon && (
        <div
          style={{
            width: 56,
            height: 56,
            borderRadius: '50%',
            background: 'var(--color-bg)',
            border: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
          }}
        >
          {icon}
        </div>
      )}
      <div>
        <p style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 4 }}>
          {title}
        </p>
        {description && (
          <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', maxWidth: 360 }}>
            {description}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
      {action && <div>{action}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ui/EventCard.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Calendar, MapPin, Users, Wifi } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Event } from '../../types';
import { eventTypeConfig, formatDate, getDaysUntil } from '../../utils';
import { StatusBadge } from './StatusBadge';

interface EventCardProps {
  event: Event;
  href?: string;
  registered?: boolean;
}

export function EventCard({ event, href, registered = false }: EventCardProps) {
  const typeCfg = eventTypeConfig[event.type];
  const daysUntil = getDaysUntil(event.startDate);
  const spotsLeft = event.maxCapacity
    ? event.maxCapacity - event.registeredIds.length
    : null;

  const content = (
    <div
      className="card card-hover"
      style={{ padding: 20, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 14, height: '100%' }}
    >
      {/* Type badge + countdown */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 8 }}>
        <StatusBadge {...typeCfg} size="sm" dot={false} />
        {daysUntil > 0 ? (
          <span
            style={{
              fontSize: 11,
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: daysUntil <= 7 ? 'var(--color-error)' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
            }}
          >
            {daysUntil === 1 ? 'Tomorrow' : `In ${daysUntil} days`}
          </span>
        ) : daysUntil === 0 ? (
          <span style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-success)' }}>Today!</span>
        ) : (
          <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>Past event</span>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Title */}
      <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)', lineHeight: 1.4 }}>
        {event.title}
      </h3>

      {/* Description */}
      <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.55, flex: 1 }}>
        {event.description.length > 110
          ? event.description.slice(0, 110) + '…'
          : event.description}
      </p>

      {/* Meta */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6 }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
          <Calendar size={13} style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
          {formatDate(event.startDate)}
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
          {event.isOnline ? <Wifi size={13} style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} /> : <MapPin size={13} style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />}
          {event.location}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Footer */}
      <div
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          paddingTop: 10,
          borderTop: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
        }}
      >
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
          <Users size={12} />
          {event.registeredIds.length} registered
          {spotsLeft !== null && spotsLeft <= 10 && spotsLeft > 0 && (
            <span style={{ color: 'var(--color-warning)', fontWeight: 500, marginLeft: 4 }}>
              · {spotsLeft} spots left
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        {registered && (
          <span
            style={{
              fontSize: 11,
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: 'var(--color-success)',
              background: '#F0FDF4',
              padding: '2px 8px',
              borderRadius: 4,
            }}
          >
            ✓ Registered
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  if (href) {
    return (
      <Link to={href} style={{ display: 'block', height: '100%', textDecoration: 'none' }}>
        {content}
      </Link>
    );
  }

  return content;
}

import React from 'react';
import { cn } from '../../utils';

interface FilterOption {
  value: string;
  label: string;
  count?: number;
}

interface FilterBarProps {
  options: FilterOption[];
  selected: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  label?: string;
}

export function FilterBar({ options, selected, onChange, label }: FilterBarProps) {
  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      aria-label={label ?? 'Filter options'}
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        gap: 6,
        flexWrap: 'wrap',
        alignItems: 'center',
      }}
    >
      {options.map((opt) => (
        <button
          key={opt.value}
          onClick={() => onChange(opt.value)}
          aria-pressed={selected === opt.value}
          style={{
            padding: '6px 14px',
            borderRadius: 99,
            fontSize: 13,
            fontWeight: 500,
            border: '1px solid',
            cursor: 'pointer',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: 6,
            transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
            background:
              selected === opt.value ? 'var(--color-primary)' : 'var(--color-surface)',
            color:
              selected === opt.value ? '#ffffff' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
            borderColor:
              selected === opt.value ? 'var(--color-primary)' : 'var(--color-border)',
          }}
        >
          {opt.label}
          {opt.count !== undefined && (
            <span
              style={{
                fontSize: 11,
                padding: '1px 6px',
                borderRadius: 99,
                background:
                  selected === opt.value ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)' : 'var(--color-bg)',
                color:
                  selected === opt.value ? '#ffffff' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
                fontWeight: 600,
              }}
            >
              {opt.count}
            </span>
          )}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

interface FormFieldProps {
  label: string;
  htmlFor?: string;
  error?: string;
  required?: boolean;
  hint?: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export function FormField({ label, htmlFor, error, required, hint, children }: FormFieldProps) {
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6 }}>
      <label htmlFor={htmlFor} className="form-label">
        {label}
        {required && <span style={{ color: 'var(--color-error)', marginLeft: 3 }}>*</span>}
      </label>
      {children}
      {hint && !error && (
        <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>{hint}</p>
      )}
      {error && (
        <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-error)' }}>{error}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ui/Modal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';

interface ModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  maxWidth?: number;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  footer?: React.ReactNode;
}

const sizeMap: Record<string, number> = { sm: 440, md: 560, lg: 720 };

export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, children, maxWidth, size, footer }: ModalProps) {
  const resolvedMax = maxWidth ?? (size ? sizeMap[size] : 540);
  // Close on Escape
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handler);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', handler);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  // Prevent scroll when open
  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    } else {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = ''; };
  }, [open]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="modal-overlay"
      onClick={(e) => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose(); }}
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label={title}
    >
      <div className="modal-content" style={{ maxWidth: resolvedMax }}>
        {/* Header */}
        {title && (
          <div
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              padding: '20px 24px 16px',
              borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
            }}
          >
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 600 }}>{title}</h2>
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm"
              aria-label="Close dialog"
              style={{ padding: 6 }}
            >
              <X size={18} />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Body */}
        <div style={{ padding: '20px 24px' }}>{children}</div>

        {/* Footer */}
        {footer && (
          <div
            style={{
              padding: '16px 24px 20px',
              borderTop: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
              display: 'flex',
              gap: 10,
              justifyContent: 'flex-end',
            }}
          >
            {footer}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ui/PageHeader.tsx

import React from 'react';

interface PageHeaderProps {
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  action?: React.ReactNode;
  breadcrumb?: { label: string; href?: string }[];
}

export function PageHeader({ title, subtitle, action, breadcrumb }: PageHeaderProps) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'flex-start',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        gap: 16,
        marginBottom: 24,
        flexWrap: 'wrap',
      }}
    >
      <div>
        {breadcrumb && breadcrumb.length > 0 && (
          <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" style={{ marginBottom: 6 }}>
            <ol
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                gap: 6,
                alignItems: 'center',
                listStyle: 'none',
                margin: 0,
                padding: 0,
              }}
            >
              {breadcrumb.map((item, i) => (
                <li
                  key={i}
                  style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}
                >
                  {i > 0 && (
                    <span style={{ color: 'var(--color-border)', fontSize: 13 }}>›</span>
                  )}
                  <span
                    style={{
                      fontSize: 13,
                      color: item.href ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
                      cursor: item.href ? 'pointer' : 'default',
                    }}
                  >
                    {item.label}
                  </span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
          </nav>
        )}
        <h1 style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{title}</h1>
        {subtitle && (
          <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginTop: 4 }}>
            {subtitle}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
      {action && <div style={{ flexShrink: 0 }}>{action}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { GitBranch, Users, ExternalLink, Bookmark } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Project } from '../../types';
import { StatusBadge } from './StatusBadge';
import { projectStatusConfig } from '../../utils';
import { getUserById } from '../../data/users';
import { getInitials } from '../../utils';

interface ProjectCardProps {
  project: Project;
  href?: string;
  compact?: boolean;
}

export function ProjectCard({ project, href, compact = false }: ProjectCardProps) {
  const statusCfg = projectStatusConfig[project.status];
  const lead = getUserById(project.leadId);

  const content = (
    <div
      className="card card-hover"
      style={{
        padding: compact ? 16 : 20,
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        gap: 14,
        height: '100%',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header row */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: 10, justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
        <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap', marginBottom: 6 }}>
            <StatusBadge {...statusCfg} size="sm" />
            <span
              style={{
                fontSize: 11,
                color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
                background: 'var(--color-bg)',
                border: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                borderRadius: 4,
                padding: '1px 7px',
              }}
            >
              {project.category}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h3
            style={{
              fontSize: compact ? 14 : 15,
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: 'var(--color-text)',
              lineHeight: 1.35,
            }}
          >
            {project.title}
          </h3>
        </div>
        {project.incubationReferred && (
          <span title="Incubated at Crieya" style={{ color: '#8B5CF6', flexShrink: 0 }}>
            <Bookmark size={16} fill="#8B5CF6" />
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Description */}
      {!compact && (
        <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
          {project.description.length > 120
            ? project.description.slice(0, 120) + '…'
            : project.description}
        </p>
      )}

      {/* Tags */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        {project.tags.slice(0, 4).map((tag) => (
          <span
            key={tag}
            style={{
              fontSize: 11,
              color: 'var(--color-accent)',
              background: 'var(--color-accent-light)',
              borderRadius: 4,
              padding: '2px 8px',
              fontWeight: 500,
            }}
          >
            {tag}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Footer */}
      <div
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          marginTop: 'auto',
          paddingTop: 10,
          borderTop: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
        }}
      >
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
          <div
            style={{
              width: 24,
              height: 24,
              borderRadius: '50%',
              background: 'var(--color-primary)',
              color: '#fff',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              fontSize: 10,
              fontWeight: 600,
              flexShrink: 0,
            }}
          >
            {lead ? getInitials(lead.name) : '?'}
          </div>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
            {lead?.name ?? 'Unknown'}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
          <span
            style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}
          >
            <Users size={12} />
            {project.memberIds.length}
          </span>
          {project.githubUrl && (
            <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
              <GitBranch size={12} />
            </span>
          )}
          {project.isOpenForApplications && (
            <span
              style={{
                fontSize: 11,
                color: 'var(--color-success)',
                background: '#F0FDF4',
                borderRadius: 4,
                padding: '2px 7px',
                fontWeight: 500,
              }}
            >
              Hiring
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  if (href) {
    return (
      <Link to={href} style={{ display: 'block', height: '100%', textDecoration: 'none' }}>
        {content}
      </Link>
    );
  }

  return content;
}

import React from 'react';
import { Search } from 'lucide-react';

interface SearchInputProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> {
  value: string;
  onChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void;
}

export function SearchInput({ value, onChange, placeholder = 'Search…', ...props }: SearchInputProps) {
  return (
    <div className="search-wrapper" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', position: 'relative' }}>
      <span className="search-icon" style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 12, pointerEvents: 'none', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
        <Search size={16} />
      </span>
      <input
        type="text"
        value={value}
        onChange={onChange}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        className="form-input search-input"
        style={{ paddingLeft: 38 }}
        {...props}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ui/StatCard.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { TrendingUp, TrendingDown } from 'lucide-react';

interface StatCardProps {
  label: string;
  value: string | number;
  icon?: React.ReactNode;
  trend?: { value: number; label: string };
  color?: string;
  description?: string;
}

export function StatCard({ label, value, icon, trend, color = 'var(--color-accent)', description }: StatCardProps) {
  return (
    <div className="stat-card">
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 12 }}>
        <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', fontWeight: 500, marginBottom: 6 }}>
            {label}
          </p>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', lineHeight: 1.1 }}>
            {value}
          </p>
          {description && (
            <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginTop: 4 }}>
              {description}
            </p>
          )}
          {trend && (
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: 4,
                marginTop: 8,
                fontSize: 12,
                color: trend.value >= 0 ? 'var(--color-success)' : 'var(--color-error)',
                fontWeight: 500,
              }}
            >
              {trend.value >= 0 ? <TrendingUp size={13} /> : <TrendingDown size={13} />}
              <span>{Math.abs(trend.value)}% {trend.label}</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        {icon && (
          <div
            style={{
              width: 44,
              height: 44,
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
              background: `${color}18`,
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              color,
              flexShrink: 0,
            }}
          >
            {icon}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/components/ui/StatusBadge.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';

interface StatusBadgeProps {
  label: string;
  color: string;
  bg: string;
  dot?: boolean;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
}

export function StatusBadge({ label, color, bg, dot = true, size = 'md' }: StatusBadgeProps) {
  return (
    <span
      className="badge"
      style={{
        color,
        backgroundColor: bg,
        fontSize: size === 'sm' ? '11px' : '12px',
        padding: size === 'sm' ? '2px 8px' : '3px 10px',
      }}
    >
      {dot && (
        <span
          style={{
            width: 6,
            height: 6,
            borderRadius: '50%',
            backgroundColor: color,
            display: 'inline-block',
            flexShrink: 0,
          }}
        />
      )}
      {label}
    </span>
  );
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/components/ui/Toast.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { CheckCircle, XCircle, AlertTriangle, Info, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useToast } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import type { ToastVariant } from '../../types';

const icons: Record<ToastVariant, React.ReactNode> = {
  success: <CheckCircle size={18} color="var(--color-success)" />,
  error: <XCircle size={18} color="var(--color-error)" />,
  warning: <AlertTriangle size={18} color="var(--color-warning)" />,
  info: <Info size={18} color="var(--color-accent)" />,
};

const colors: Record<ToastVariant, string> = {
  success: 'var(--color-success)',
  error: 'var(--color-error)',
  warning: 'var(--color-warning)',
  info: 'var(--color-accent)',
};

export function ToastContainer() {
  const { toasts, removeToast } = useToast();

  return (
    <div className="toast-container" role="region" aria-label="Notifications">
      {toasts.map((toast) => (
        <div
          key={toast.id}
          className="toast animate-slide-in"
          role="alert"
          aria-live="assertive"
          style={{ borderLeft: `3px solid ${colors[toast.variant]}` }}
        >
          <span style={{ display: 'flex', flexShrink: 0, marginTop: 1 }}>
            {icons[toast.variant]}
          </span>
          <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>
              {toast.title}
            </p>
            {toast.message && (
              <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginTop: 2 }}>
                {toast.message}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => removeToast(toast.id)}
            aria-label="Dismiss notification"
            style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', padding: 4, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', cursor: 'pointer', flexShrink: 0 }}
          >
            <X size={14} />
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/components/DemoRoleSwitcher.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronUp, UserCircle2, GraduationCap, Shield } from 'lucide-react';
import { useDemoRole } from '../hooks/useAppContext';
import type { Role } from '../types';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { getInitials } from '../utils';

const roles: { role: Role; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode; path: string; description: string }[] = [
  {
    role: 'student',
    label: 'Student',
    icon: <GraduationCap size={16} />,
    path: '/student/dashboard',
    description: 'Arjun Sharma · CS Year 3',
  },
  {
    role: 'faculty',
    label: 'Faculty',
    icon: <UserCircle2 size={16} />,
    path: '/faculty/dashboard',
    description: 'Dr. Meena Iyer · CS Dept.',
  },
  {
    role: 'admin',
    label: 'Admin',
    icon: <Shield size={16} />,
    path: '/admin/dashboard',
    description: 'Aditya Kumar · IT Club Admin',
  },
];

export function DemoRoleSwitcher() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const { currentRole, currentUser, switchRole } = useDemoRole();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const activeRoleConfig = roles.find((r) => r.role === currentRole)!;

  function handleSwitch(role: Role, path: string) {
    switchRole(role);
    setOpen(false);
    // Navigate to the appropriate dashboard when switching roles
    navigate(path);
  }

  return (
    <div className="demo-switcher">
      {/* Panel */}
      {open && (
        <div className="demo-switcher-panel" style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
          <div style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 4 }}>
              🎭 Demo Mode
            </p>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.4 }}>
              Switch roles to explore different experiences. No real auth.
            </p>
          </div>
          <hr className="divider" style={{ marginBottom: 12 }} />
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
            {roles.map((r) => (
              <button
                key={r.role}
                onClick={() => handleSwitch(r.role, r.path)}
                aria-pressed={currentRole === r.role}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  gap: 10,
                  padding: '8px 10px',
                  borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
                  border: '1px solid',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  textAlign: 'left',
                  width: '100%',
                  transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                  background: currentRole === r.role ? 'var(--color-primary)' : 'transparent',
                  borderColor: currentRole === r.role ? 'var(--color-primary)' : 'transparent',
                  color: currentRole === r.role ? '#ffffff' : 'var(--color-text)',
                  fontFamily: 'inherit',
                }}
              >
                <span style={{ opacity: 0.85 }}>{r.icon}</span>
                <div>
                  <p style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, margin: 0 }}>{r.label}</p>
                  <p style={{ fontSize: 11, opacity: 0.75, margin: 0, marginTop: 1 }}>{r.description}</p>
                </div>
                {currentRole === r.role && (
                  <span style={{ marginLeft: 'auto', fontSize: 11, opacity: 0.8 }}>Active</span>
                )}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Toggle Button */}
      <button
        onClick={() => setOpen((p) => !p)}
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-label="Toggle demo role switcher"
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: 8,
          padding: '8px 14px',
          background: 'var(--color-primary)',
          color: '#ffffff',
          border: 'none',
          borderRadius: 'var(--radius-xl)',
          cursor: 'pointer',
          fontFamily: 'inherit',
          fontSize: 13,
          fontWeight: 600,
          boxShadow: '0 4px 16px rgba(23, 54, 93, 0.35)',
          transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
        }}
      >
        <span
          style={{
            width: 24,
            height: 24,
            borderRadius: '50%',
            background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            fontSize: 10,
            fontWeight: 700,
          }}
        >
          {getInitials(currentUser.name)}
        </span>
        🎭 Demo: {activeRoleConfig.label}
        <ChevronUp
          size={14}
          style={{ transform: open ? 'rotate(0deg)' : 'rotate(180deg)', transition: 'transform 0.2s ease' }}
        />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/hooks/useAppContext.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Role, User, ToastMessage, ToastVariant } from '../types';
import { demoStudentUser, demoFacultyUser, demoAdminUser } from '../data/users';

// ─── Demo Role Context ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface DemoRoleContextType {
  currentUser: User;
  currentRole: Role;
  switchRole: (role: Role) => void;
}

const DemoRoleContext = createContext<DemoRoleContextType | null>(null);

export function DemoRoleProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [currentRole, setCurrentRole] = useState<Role>('student');

  const userMap: Record<Role, User> = {
    student: demoStudentUser,
    faculty: demoFacultyUser,
    admin: demoAdminUser,
  };

  const switchRole = useCallback((role: Role) => {
    setCurrentRole(role);
  }, []);

  return (
    <DemoRoleContext.Provider
      value={{ currentUser: userMap[currentRole], currentRole, switchRole }}
    >
      {children}
    </DemoRoleContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useDemoRole(): DemoRoleContextType {
  const ctx = useContext(DemoRoleContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useDemoRole must be used within DemoRoleProvider');
  return ctx;
}

// ─── Toast Context ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface ToastContextType {
  toasts: ToastMessage[];
  addToast: (variant: ToastVariant, title: string, message?: string) => void;
  removeToast: (id: string) => void;
}

const ToastContext = createContext<ToastContextType | null>(null);

export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<ToastMessage[]>([]);

  const addToast = useCallback(
    (variant: ToastVariant, title: string, message?: string) => {
      const id = `toast-${Date.now()}`;
      setToasts((prev) => [...prev, { id, variant, title, message }]);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
      }, 4000);
    },
    []
  );

  const removeToast = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
  }, []);

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={{ toasts, addToast, removeToast }}>
      {children}
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useToast(): ToastContextType {
  const ctx = useContext(ToastContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useToast must be used within ToastProvider');
  return ctx;
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/hooks/useAppStore.tsx
================================================================================
/**
 * useAppStore — Global reactive demo store
 * Manages ideas, applications, tasks, notifications, registrations, and quiz results
 * so interactions across pages stay in sync.
 */
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';
import type { Idea, ProjectApplication, Task, Notification } from '../types';
import { mockIdeas } from '../data/mock';
import { mockApplications, mockTasks, mockNotifications } from '../data/mock';

interface QuizResult {
  quizId: string;
  score: number;
  total: number;
  completedAt: string;
}

interface AppStoreContextType {
  // Ideas
  ideas: Idea[];
  submitIdea: (idea: Idea) => void;
  updateIdeaStatus: (id: string, status: Idea['status'], reviewNote?: string) => void;
  voteIdea: (id: string, userId: string) => void;

  // Applications
  applications: ProjectApplication[];
  submitApplication: (app: ProjectApplication) => void;
  updateApplicationStatus: (id: string, status: ProjectApplication['status']) => void;

  // Tasks
  tasks: Task[];
  updateTaskStatus: (id: string, status: Task['status']) => void;

  // Notifications
  notifications: Notification[];
  markNotificationRead: (id: string) => void;
  markAllRead: () => void;
  addNotification: (notif: Notification) => void;

  // Event registrations (set of event ids the current user has registered)
  registeredEventIds: Set<string>;
  registerEvent: (eventId: string) => void;
  unregisterEvent: (eventId: string) => void;

  // Quiz results
  quizResults: QuizResult[];
  saveQuizResult: (result: QuizResult) => void;

  // Announcements
  announcements: { id: string; title: string; body: string; createdAt: string; audience: string }[];
  addAnnouncement: (a: { title: string; body: string; audience: string }) => void;
}

const AppStoreContext = createContext<AppStoreContextType | null>(null);

const LS_KEY = 'itclub_demo_store';

function loadFromLS<T>(key: string, fallback: T): T {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
    if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
  } catch {}
  return fallback;
}

export function AppStoreProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [ideas, setIdeas] = useState<Idea[]>(() =>
    loadFromLS(`${LS_KEY}_ideas`, mockIdeas)
  );
  const [applications, setApplications] = useState<ProjectApplication[]>(() =>
    loadFromLS(`${LS_KEY}_apps`, mockApplications)
  );
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<Task[]>(() =>
    loadFromLS(`${LS_KEY}_tasks`, mockTasks)
  );
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<Notification[]>(() =>
    loadFromLS(`${LS_KEY}_notifs`, mockNotifications)
  );
  const [registeredEventIds, setRegisteredEventIds] = useState<Set<string>>(
    () => new Set<string>(loadFromLS<string[]>(`${LS_KEY}_events`, ['e1', 'e3']))
  );
  const [quizResults, setQuizResults] = useState<QuizResult[]>(() =>
    loadFromLS(`${LS_KEY}_quizzes`, [])
  );
  const [announcements, setAnnouncements] = useState<
    { id: string; title: string; body: string; createdAt: string; audience: string }[]
  >(() =>
    loadFromLS(`${LS_KEY}_announcements`, [
      {
        id: 'ann1',
        title: 'InnoHack 2026 Registrations Open!',
        body: 'Teams of 2-4 can now register for InnoHack 2026. Last date: Nov 10. Prizes worth ₹2 Lakhs!',
        createdAt: '2026-10-20T10:00:00',
        audience: 'all',
      },
    ])
  );

  // Persist to localStorage on change
  useEffect(() => { try { localStorage.setItem(`${LS_KEY}_ideas`, JSON.stringify(ideas)); } catch {} }, [ideas]);
  useEffect(() => { try { localStorage.setItem(`${LS_KEY}_apps`, JSON.stringify(applications)); } catch {} }, [applications]);
  useEffect(() => { try { localStorage.setItem(`${LS_KEY}_tasks`, JSON.stringify(tasks)); } catch {} }, [tasks]);
  useEffect(() => { try { localStorage.setItem(`${LS_KEY}_notifs`, JSON.stringify(notifications)); } catch {} }, [notifications]);
  useEffect(() => { try { localStorage.setItem(`${LS_KEY}_events`, JSON.stringify([...registeredEventIds])); } catch {} }, [registeredEventIds]);
  useEffect(() => { try { localStorage.setItem(`${LS_KEY}_quizzes`, JSON.stringify(quizResults)); } catch {} }, [quizResults]);
  useEffect(() => { try { localStorage.setItem(`${LS_KEY}_announcements`, JSON.stringify(announcements)); } catch {} }, [announcements]);

  const submitIdea = useCallback((idea: Idea) => {
    setIdeas((prev) => [idea, ...prev]);
  }, []);

  const updateIdeaStatus = useCallback((id: string, status: Idea['status'], reviewNote?: string) => {
    setIdeas((prev) =>
      prev.map((i) => (i.id === id ? { ...i, status, reviewNote: reviewNote ?? i.reviewNote } : i))
    );
  }, []);

  const voteIdea = useCallback((id: string, userId: string) => {
    setIdeas((prev) =>
      prev.map((i) => {
        if (i.id !== id) return i;
        const hasVoted = i.votedBy.includes(userId);
        return {
          ...i,
          votes: hasVoted ? i.votes - 1 : i.votes + 1,
          votedBy: hasVoted ? i.votedBy.filter((v) => v !== userId) : [...i.votedBy, userId],
        };
      })
    );
  }, []);

  const submitApplication = useCallback((app: ProjectApplication) => {
    setApplications((prev) => [app, ...prev]);
  }, []);

  const updateApplicationStatus = useCallback((id: string, status: ProjectApplication['status']) => {
    setApplications((prev) => prev.map((a) => (a.id === id ? { ...a, status } : a)));
  }, []);

  const updateTaskStatus = useCallback((id: string, status: Task['status']) => {
    setTasks((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, status } : t)));
  }, []);

  const markNotificationRead = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setNotifications((prev) => prev.map((n) => (n.id === id ? { ...n, read: true } : n)));
  }, []);

  const markAllRead = useCallback(() => {
    setNotifications((prev) => prev.map((n) => ({ ...n, read: true })));
  }, []);

  const addNotification = useCallback((notif: Notification) => {
    setNotifications((prev) => [notif, ...prev]);
  }, []);

  const registerEvent = useCallback((eventId: string) => {
    setRegisteredEventIds((prev) => new Set([...prev, eventId]));
  }, []);

  const unregisterEvent = useCallback((eventId: string) => {
    setRegisteredEventIds((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      next.delete(eventId);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const saveQuizResult = useCallback((result: QuizResult) => {
    setQuizResults((prev) => [result, ...prev.filter((r) => r.quizId !== result.quizId)]);
  }, []);

  const addAnnouncement = useCallback((a: { title: string; body: string; audience: string }) => {
    const newAnn = { ...a, id: `ann${Date.now()}`, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() };
    setAnnouncements((prev) => [newAnn, ...prev]);
  }, []);

  return (
    <AppStoreContext.Provider
      value={{
        ideas, submitIdea, updateIdeaStatus, voteIdea,
        applications, submitApplication, updateApplicationStatus,
        tasks, updateTaskStatus,
        notifications, markNotificationRead, markAllRead, addNotification,
        registeredEventIds, registerEvent, unregisterEvent,
        quizResults, saveQuizResult,
        announcements, addAnnouncement,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AppStoreContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAppStore(): AppStoreContextType {
  const ctx = useContext(AppStoreContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAppStore must be used within AppStoreProvider');
  return ctx;
}

FILE: src/layouts/DashboardLayout.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { NavLink, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  LayoutDashboard, FolderKanban, Lightbulb, Calendar, BookOpen,
  Users, Bell, Menu, Code2, MessageSquare, UserSearch,
  ClipboardList, UserCheck, BarChart3, GraduationCap, Megaphone
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useDemoRole } from '../hooks/useAppContext';
import { useAppStore } from '../hooks/useAppStore';
import { getInitials, timeAgo } from '../utils';

type NavItem = {
  href: string;
  label: string;
  icon: React.ReactNode;
};

const studentNavItems: NavItem[] = [
  { href: '/student/dashboard', label: 'Dashboard', icon: <LayoutDashboard size={18} /> },
  { href: '/student/projects', label: 'My Projects', icon: <FolderKanban size={18} /> },
  { href: '/student/ideas', label: 'Ideas', icon: <Lightbulb size={18} /> },
  { href: '/student/applications', label: 'Applications', icon: <ClipboardList size={18} /> },
  { href: '/student/teammates', label: 'Find Teammates', icon: <UserSearch size={18} /> },
  { href: '/student/chat', label: 'Project Chat', icon: <MessageSquare size={18} /> },
  { href: '/student/events', label: 'Events', icon: <Calendar size={18} /> },
  { href: '/student/learn', label: 'Learn', icon: <BookOpen size={18} /> },
  { href: '/student/sessions', label: 'Sessions', icon: <UserCheck size={18} /> },
];

const facultyNavItems: NavItem[] = [
  { href: '/faculty/dashboard', label: 'Dashboard', icon: <LayoutDashboard size={18} /> },
  { href: '/faculty/projects', label: 'Projects', icon: <FolderKanban size={18} /> },
  { href: '/faculty/sessions', label: 'Sessions', icon: <UserCheck size={18} /> },
  { href: '/faculty/ideas', label: 'Review Ideas', icon: <Lightbulb size={18} /> },
  { href: '/faculty/students', label: 'My Students', icon: <GraduationCap size={18} /> },
];

const adminNavItems: NavItem[] = [
  { href: '/admin/dashboard', label: 'Dashboard', icon: <LayoutDashboard size={18} /> },
  { href: '/admin/users', label: 'Users', icon: <Users size={18} /> },
  { href: '/admin/projects', label: 'Projects', icon: <FolderKanban size={18} /> },
  { href: '/admin/ideas', label: 'Ideas', icon: <Lightbulb size={18} /> },
  { href: '/admin/events', label: 'Events', icon: <Calendar size={18} /> },
  { href: '/admin/analytics', label: 'Analytics', icon: <BarChart3 size={18} /> },
  { href: '/admin/announcements', label: 'Announcements', icon: <Megaphone size={18} /> },
];

interface DashboardLayoutProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  title?: string;
}

export function DashboardLayout({ children, title }: DashboardLayoutProps) {
  const { currentUser, currentRole } = useDemoRole();
  const { notifications, markNotificationRead, markAllRead } = useAppStore();
  const [sidebarOpen, setSidebarOpen] = useState(false);
  const [notifOpen, setNotifOpen] = useState(false);

  const navItems =
    currentRole === 'student'
      ? studentNavItems
      : currentRole === 'faculty'
      ? facultyNavItems
      : adminNavItems;

  const roleLabel =
    currentRole === 'student'
      ? 'Student Portal'
      : currentRole === 'faculty'
      ? 'Faculty Portal'
      : 'Admin Panel';

  const userNotifs = notifications.filter((n) => n.userId === currentUser.id);
  const unreadCount = userNotifs.filter((n) => !n.read).length;

  return (
    <div className="dashboard-layout">
      {/* Sidebar overlay for mobile */}
      {sidebarOpen && (
        <div
          onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}
          style={{
            position: 'fixed',
            inset: 0,
            background: 'rgba(16,39,67,0.4)',
            zIndex: 49,
            display: 'none',
          }}
          className="sidebar-overlay"
        />
      )}

      {/* Sidebar */}
      <aside
        className={`sidebar ${sidebarOpen ? 'open' : ''}`}
        aria-label="Sidebar navigation"
      >
        {/* Logo */}
        <div
          style={{
            padding: '20px 20px 16px',
            borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
          }}
        >
          <Link
            to="/"
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              gap: 10,
              textDecoration: 'none',
            }}
          >
            <div
              style={{
                width: 34,
                height: 34,
                borderRadius: 9,
                background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
              }}
            >
              <Code2 size={18} color="#fff" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: '#fff', fontSize: 14, margin: 0, lineHeight: 1.2 }}>
                IT Club
              </p>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.6)', margin: 0 }}>{roleLabel}</p>
            </div>
          </Link>
        </div>

        {/* User info */}
        <div style={{ padding: '14px 20px', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)' }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
            <div
              style={{
                width: 38,
                height: 38,
                borderRadius: '50%',
                background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                fontSize: 14,
                fontWeight: 700,
                color: '#fff',
                flexShrink: 0,
              }}
            >
              {getInitials(currentUser.name)}
            </div>
            <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
              <p
                style={{
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  color: '#fff',
                  margin: 0,
                  overflow: 'hidden',
                  textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                  whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                }}
              >
                {currentUser.name}
              </p>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.6)', margin: 0 }}>
                {currentUser.department}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Nav Items */}
        <nav
          aria-label="Dashboard navigation"
          style={{ flex: 1, padding: '12px 12px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 2 }}
        >
          {navItems.map((item) => (
            <NavLink
              key={item.href}
              to={item.href}
              onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}
              style={({ isActive }) => ({
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: 10,
                padding: '9px 12px',
                borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
                fontSize: 13,
                fontWeight: isActive ? 600 : 400,
                color: isActive ? '#fff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)',
                background: isActive ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)' : 'transparent',
                textDecoration: 'none',
                transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
              })}
            >
              {item.icon}
              {item.label}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>

        {/* Bottom Actions */}
        <div
          style={{
            padding: '12px 12px',
            borderTop: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: 2,
          }}
        >
          <Link
            to="/"
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              gap: 10,
              padding: '9px 12px',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
              fontSize: 13,
              color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)',
              textDecoration: 'none',
              transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
            }}
          >
            <Code2 size={18} />
            Public Site
          </Link>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* Main */}
      <div className="main-content">
        {/* Top Bar */}
        <header
          style={{
            height: 60,
            background: 'var(--color-surface)',
            borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            padding: '0 24px',
            gap: 16,
            position: 'sticky',
            top: 0,
            zIndex: 30,
          }}
        >
          {/* Mobile sidebar toggle */}
          <button
            className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm sidebar-toggle"
            onClick={() => setSidebarOpen((p) => !p)}
            aria-label="Toggle sidebar"
            style={{ display: 'none' }}
          >
            <Menu size={20} />
          </button>

          <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
            {title && (
              <h1 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)', margin: 0 }}>
                {title}
              </h1>
            )}
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
            {/* Notifications */}
            <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
              <button
                className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm"
                onClick={() => setNotifOpen((p) => !p)}
                aria-label={`Notifications (${unreadCount} unread)`}
                style={{ position: 'relative', padding: 8 }}
              >
                <Bell size={18} />
                {unreadCount > 0 && (
                  <span
                    style={{
                      position: 'absolute',
                      top: 4,
                      right: 4,
                      minWidth: 16,
                      height: 16,
                      borderRadius: 99,
                      background: 'var(--color-error)',
                      border: '2px solid var(--color-surface)',
                      fontSize: 9,
                      fontWeight: 700,
                      color: '#fff',
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                    }}
                  >
                    {unreadCount > 9 ? '9+' : unreadCount}
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>

              {notifOpen && (
                <>
                  <div
                    style={{ position: 'fixed', inset: 0, zIndex: 28 }}
                    onClick={() => setNotifOpen(false)}
                  />
                  <div
                    style={{
                      position: 'absolute',
                      right: 0,
                      top: 'calc(100% + 8px)',
                      width: 360,
                      background: 'var(--color-surface)',
                      border: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                      borderRadius: 'var(--radius-lg)',
                      boxShadow: 'var(--shadow-elevated)',
                      zIndex: 29,
                      animation: 'scaleIn 0.15s ease',
                      transformOrigin: 'top right',
                    }}
                  >
                    <div
                      style={{
                        padding: '14px 16px',
                        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                      }}
                    >
                      <p style={{ fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 14 }}>Notifications</p>
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center' }}>
                        {unreadCount > 0 && (
                          <>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: 11, background: 'var(--color-error)', color: '#fff', padding: '2px 7px', borderRadius: 99 }}>
                              {unreadCount} new
                            </span>
                            <button
                              onClick={markAllRead}
                              style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--color-accent)', background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', fontFamily: 'inherit', padding: 0 }}
                            >
                              Mark all read
                            </button>
                          </>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ maxHeight: 340, overflowY: 'auto' }}>
                      {userNotifs.slice(0, 6).map((notif) => (
                        <div
                          key={notif.id}
                          onClick={() => markNotificationRead(notif.id)}
                          style={{
                            padding: '12px 16px',
                            borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                            background: notif.read ? 'transparent' : 'var(--color-accent-light)',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            transition: 'background 0.2s ease',
                          }}
                        >
                          <p style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: notif.read ? 400 : 600, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 2 }}>
                            {!notif.read && <span style={{ display: 'inline-block', width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'var(--color-accent)', marginRight: 6, marginBottom: 1 }} />}
                            {notif.title}
                          </p>
                          <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.45 }}>
                            {notif.message}
                          </p>
                          <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginTop: 4 }}>
                            {timeAgo(notif.createdAt)}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                      {userNotifs.length === 0 && (
                        <div style={{ padding: 24, textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', fontSize: 13 }}>
                          🔔 No notifications yet
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* User avatar */}
            <div
              style={{
                width: 32,
                height: 32,
                borderRadius: '50%',
                background: 'var(--color-primary)',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 700,
                color: '#fff',
                cursor: 'pointer',
              }}
              title={currentUser.name}
            >
              {getInitials(currentUser.name)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* Page Content */}
        <main style={{ flex: 1, padding: '28px 28px', background: 'var(--color-bg)' }}>
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>

      <style>{`
        @media (max-width: 768px) {
          .sidebar-toggle { display: flex !important; }
          .sidebar-overlay { display: block !important; }
        }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  );
}

import { PrimaryButton } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { useDemoRole } from '../hooks/useAppContext';
import { getInitials } from '../utils';

const navLinks = [
  { href: '/', label: 'Home' },
  { href: '/projects', label: 'Projects' },
  { href: '/events', label: 'Events' },
  { href: '/learn', label: 'Learn' },
  { href: '/ideas', label: 'Ideas' },
  { href: '/workflow', label: 'Workflow' },
  { href: '/incubation', label: 'Incubation' },
];

export function PublicLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const { currentRole, currentUser } = useDemoRole();

  const dashboardPath =
    currentRole === 'student'
      ? '/student/dashboard'

      ? '/faculty/dashboard'

      {/* Header */}

            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            gap: 24,
          }}
        >
          {/* Logo */}

            to="/"

              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',

                {link.label}

          {/* Actions */}

              className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm mobile-menu-btn"

              aria-expanded={mobileOpen}
              style={{ display: 'none' }}

FILE: src/pages/admin/AdminAnalyticsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { StatCard } from '../../components/ui/StatCard';
import { BarChart3, TrendingUp, Users, FolderKanban } from 'lucide-react';
import { mockProjects } from '../../data/projects';
import { mockUsers } from '../../data/users';
import { mockEvents, mockIdeas } from '../../data/mock';
import {
  BarChart, Bar, XAxis, YAxis, CartesianGrid, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer, PieChart, Pie, Cell, Legend
} from 'recharts';

const projectsByCategory = Object.entries(
  mockProjects.reduce((acc, p) => {
    acc[p.category] = (acc[p.category] || 0) + 1;
    return acc;
  }, {} as Record<string, number>)
).map(([name, value]) => ({ name: name.replace(' / ', '/'), value }));

const usersByRole = [
  { name: 'Students', value: mockUsers.filter((u) => u.role === 'student').length },
  { name: 'Faculty', value: mockUsers.filter((u) => u.role === 'faculty').length },
  { name: 'Admins', value: mockUsers.filter((u) => u.role === 'admin').length },
];

const COLORS = ['#3678B7', '#22C55E', '#8B5CF6', '#F59E0B', '#EF4444'];

export function AdminAnalyticsPage() {
  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Analytics">
      <PageHeader title="Analytics" subtitle="Platform usage metrics and insights." />
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(180px, 1fr))', gap: 16, marginBottom: 28 }}>
        <StatCard label="Total Users" value={mockUsers.length} icon={<Users size={20} />} color="var(--color-accent)" trend={{ value: 12, label: 'this month' }} />
        <StatCard label="Total Projects" value={mockProjects.length} icon={<FolderKanban size={20} />} color="var(--color-primary)" trend={{ value: 8, label: 'this semester' }} />
        <StatCard label="Ideas Submitted" value={mockIdeas.length} icon={<TrendingUp size={20} />} color="var(--color-warning)" />
        <StatCard label="Events Held" value={mockEvents.length} icon={<BarChart3 size={20} />} color="var(--color-success)" />
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 20 }}>
        <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 20 }}>Projects by Category</h2>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={240}>
            <BarChart data={projectsByCategory} margin={{ top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 20, left: -20 }}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="var(--color-border)" />
              <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }} angle={-30} textAnchor="end" />
              <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }} />
              <Tooltip contentStyle={{ background: 'var(--color-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--color-border)', borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 13 }} />
              <Bar dataKey="value" fill="var(--color-accent)" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
            </BarChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </div>
        <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 20 }}>Users by Role</h2>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={240}>
            <PieChart>
              <Pie data={usersByRole} cx="50%" cy="50%" outerRadius={80} dataKey="value" label={({ name, value }) => `${name}: ${value}`} labelLine={false}>
                {usersByRole.map((_, index) => <Cell key={index} fill={COLORS[index % COLORS.length]} />)}
              </Pie>
              <Tooltip contentStyle={{ background: 'var(--color-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--color-border)', borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 13 }} />
              <Legend />
            </PieChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </div>
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/admin/AdminAnnouncementsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { FormField } from '../../components/ui/FormField';
import { useAppStore } from '../../hooks/useAppStore';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { useToast } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { formatDateTime } from '../../utils';
import { Megaphone, Plus, Users, BookOpen, Bell, Send } from 'lucide-react';

const AUDIENCE_OPTIONS = ['all', 'students', 'faculty', 'admin'];

export function AdminAnnouncementsPage() {
  const { announcements, addAnnouncement } = useAppStore();
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { addToast } = useToast();

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ title: '', body: '', audience: 'all' });
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  function handleCreate() {
    if (!form.title.trim() || !form.body.trim()) {
      addToast('error', 'Required fields missing', 'Please fill in the title and body.');
      return;
    }
    setSubmitting(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      addAnnouncement(form);
      setForm({ title: '', body: '', audience: 'all' });
      setShowForm(false);
      setSubmitting(false);
      addToast('success', 'Announcement Posted!', 'All targeted members will see this in their notifications.');
    }, 700);
  }

  const audienceIcons: Record<string, React.ReactNode> = {
    all: <Bell size={14} />,
    students: <BookOpen size={14} />,
    faculty: <Users size={14} />,
    admin: <Megaphone size={14} />,
  };

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Announcements">
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 780 }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 24 }}>
          <div>
            <h1 style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 4 }}>Announcements</h1>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
              Broadcast messages to students, faculty, or all members.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => setShowForm((p) => !p)} style={{ gap: 6 }}>
            <Plus size={15} /> New Announcement
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* New Announcement Form */}
        {showForm && (
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 24, marginBottom: 24, border: '2px solid var(--color-accent)' }}>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 16, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
              <Megaphone size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }} /> Create Announcement
            </h2>
            <FormField label="Title" required>
              <input className="input" placeholder="Announcement title" value={form.title} onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, title: e.target.value }))} />
            </FormField>
            <FormField label="Message Body" required>
              <textarea
                className="input"
                rows={4}
                placeholder="Write your announcement here..."
                value={form.body}
                onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, body: e.target.value }))}
                style={{ resize: 'vertical' }}
              />
            </FormField>
            <FormField label="Target Audience">
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10 }}>
                {AUDIENCE_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                  <button
                    key={opt}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, audience: opt }))}
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      padding: '8px 12px',
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      border: `2px solid ${form.audience === opt ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-border)'}`,
                      background: form.audience === opt ? 'var(--color-accent-light)' : 'transparent',
                      cursor: 'pointer',
                      fontFamily: 'inherit',
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: 600,
                      color: form.audience === opt ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
                      display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 6,
                      transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                      textTransform: 'capitalize',
                    }}
                  >
                    {audienceIcons[opt]} {opt}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </FormField>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, justifyContent: 'flex-end', marginTop: 16 }}>
              <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => setShowForm(false)}>Cancel</button>
              <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={handleCreate} disabled={submitting} style={{ gap: 6 }}>
                <Send size={14} /> {submitting ? 'Posting…' : 'Post Announcement'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Announcements List */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
          {announcements.map((ann) => (
            <div key={ann.id} className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', marginBottom: 10 }}>
                <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{ann.title}</h3>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flexShrink: 0, marginLeft: 12 }}>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 11, padding: '3px 10px', background: 'var(--color-accent-light)', color: 'var(--color-accent)', borderRadius: 99, fontWeight: 600, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, textTransform: 'capitalize' }}>
                    {audienceIcons[ann.audience]} {ann.audience}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.6, marginBottom: 10 }}>{ann.body}</p>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                Posted {formatDateTime(ann.createdAt)}
              </p>
            </div>
          ))}
          {announcements.length === 0 && (
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
              No announcements yet. Create your first one above.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}
