FILE: src/pages/admin/AdminDashboard.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { StatCard } from '../../components/ui/StatCard';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/StatusBadge';
import { mockProjects } from '../../data/projects';
import { mockUsers } from '../../data/users';
import { mockIdeas, mockEvents } from '../../data/mock';
import { projectStatusConfig, ideaStatusConfig, formatDate } from '../../utils';
import {
  Users, FolderKanban, Lightbulb, Calendar,
  TrendingUp, Shield, CheckCircle, Clock, Eye
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

const recentActivity = [
  { id: 1, text: 'Arjun Sharma submitted a new project: "Campus Smart Attendance"', time: '2h ago', type: 'project' },
  { id: 2, text: 'Priya Nair submitted a new idea: "Campus Carpooling App"', time: '5h ago', type: 'idea' },
  { id: 3, text: 'InnoHack 2026 now has 5 registered teams', time: '1d ago', type: 'event' },
  { id: 4, text: 'Dr. Meena Iyer approved AI Study Buddy for incubation review', time: '2d ago', type: 'review' },
  { id: 5, text: 'New member Sneha Reddy joined the platform', time: '3d ago', type: 'user' },
];

const activityColors: Record<string, string> = {
  project: 'var(--color-accent)',
  idea: 'var(--color-warning)',
  event: 'var(--color-success)',
  review: 'var(--color-incubated)',
  user: 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
};

export function AdminDashboard() {
  const totalProjects = mockProjects.length;
  const totalUsers = mockUsers.length;
  const pendingIdeas = mockIdeas.filter((i) => i.status === 'pending').length;
  const upcomingEvents = mockEvents.length;

  const projectsByStatus = Object.entries(
    mockProjects.reduce((acc, p) => {
      acc[p.status] = (acc[p.status] || 0) + 1;
      return acc;
    }, {} as Record<string, number>)
  );

  const roleBreakdown = [
    { role: 'Students', count: mockUsers.filter((u) => u.role === 'student').length, color: 'var(--color-accent)' },
    { role: 'Faculty', count: mockUsers.filter((u) => u.role === 'faculty').length, color: 'var(--color-success)' },
    { role: 'Admins', count: mockUsers.filter((u) => u.role === 'admin').length, color: 'var(--color-incubated)' },
  ];

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Admin Dashboard">
      <PageHeader
        title="Platform Overview"
        subtitle="Monitor and manage the IT Club & Innovation Hub platform."
      />

      {/* Stats */}
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(190px, 1fr))', gap: 16, marginBottom: 28 }}>
        <StatCard label="Total Users" value={totalUsers} icon={<Users size={20} />} color="var(--color-accent)" trend={{ value: 12, label: 'this month' }} />
        <StatCard label="Total Projects" value={totalProjects} icon={<FolderKanban size={20} />} color="var(--color-primary)" trend={{ value: 8, label: 'this semester' }} />
        <StatCard label="Pending Ideas" value={pendingIdeas} icon={<Lightbulb size={20} />} color="var(--color-warning)" description="Awaiting review" />
        <StatCard label="Events" value={upcomingEvents} icon={<Calendar size={20} />} color="var(--color-success)" description="This semester" />
        <StatCard label="Incubated Projects" value={mockProjects.filter(p => p.incubationReferred).length} icon={<TrendingUp size={20} />} color="var(--color-incubated)" />
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr 320px', gap: 20, alignItems: 'start' }}>
        {/* Recent Activity */}
        <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20, gridColumn: 'span 2' }}>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 16 }}>Recent Activity</h2>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 0 }}>
            {recentActivity.map((item) => (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'flex-start',
                  gap: 12,
                  padding: '12px 0',
                  borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                }}
              >
                <div
                  style={{
                    width: 8,
                    height: 8,
                    borderRadius: '50%',
                    background: activityColors[item.type],
                    marginTop: 6,
                    flexShrink: 0,
                  }}
                />
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{item.text}</p>
                  <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginTop: 2 }}>{item.time}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Column */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
          {/* Project Breakdown */}
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 14 }}>Projects by Status</h2>
            {projectsByStatus.map(([status, count]) => {
              const cfg = projectStatusConfig[status as keyof typeof projectStatusConfig];
              if (!cfg) return null;
              return (
                <div
                  key={status}
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    padding: '7px 0',
                    borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                  }}
                >
                  <StatusBadge {...cfg} size="sm" />
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{count}</span>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {/* User Breakdown */}
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 14 }}>User Breakdown</h2>
            {roleBreakdown.map((r) => (
              <div
                key={r.role}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'space-between',
                  padding: '8px 0',
                  borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                }}
              >
                <span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{r.role}</span>
                <span
                  style={{
                    fontSize: 14,
                    fontWeight: 700,
                    color: r.color,
                    background: `${r.color}18`,
                    padding: '2px 10px',
                    borderRadius: 99,
                  }}
                >
                  {r.count}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Quick Links */}
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 12 }}>Admin Actions</h2>
            {[
              { to: '/admin/users', label: 'Manage Users', icon: <Users size={14} /> },
              { to: '/admin/projects', label: 'Review Projects', icon: <FolderKanban size={14} /> },
              { to: '/admin/ideas', label: 'Approve Ideas', icon: <Lightbulb size={14} /> },
              { to: '/admin/events', label: 'Manage Events', icon: <Calendar size={14} /> },
            ].map((link) => (
              <Link
                key={link.to}
                to={link.to}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  gap: 8,
                  padding: '8px 12px',
                  borderRadius: 8,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  color: 'var(--color-text)',
                  background: 'var(--color-bg)',
                  border: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                  textDecoration: 'none',
                  marginBottom: 8,
                  transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                }}
              >
                <span style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }}>{link.icon}</span>
                {link.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/admin/AdminIdeasPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { mockIdeas } from '../../data/mock';
import { ideaStatusConfig, timeAgo } from '../../utils';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/StatusBadge';
import { getUserById } from '../../data/users';
import { ThumbsUp, CheckCircle, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { useToast } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';

export function AdminIdeasPage() {
  const { addToast } = useToast();
  const [ideas, setIdeas] = useState(mockIdeas);

  function handleApprove(id: string) {
    setIdeas((prev) => prev.map((i) => i.id === id ? { ...i, status: 'approved' as const } : i));
    addToast('success', 'Idea Approved');
  }
  function handleReject(id: string) {
    setIdeas((prev) => prev.map((i) => i.id === id ? { ...i, status: 'rejected' as const } : i));
    addToast('warning', 'Idea Rejected');
  }

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Ideas">
      <PageHeader title="Idea Management" subtitle={`${ideas.length} total ideas.`} />
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
        {ideas.map((idea) => {
          const author = getUserById(idea.submittedBy);
          const cfg = ideaStatusConfig[idea.status];
          return (
            <div key={idea.id} className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 12, marginBottom: 8 }}>
                <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 600 }}>{idea.title}</h3>
                <StatusBadge {...cfg} size="sm" />
              </div>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 12 }}>{idea.description}</p>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: idea.status === 'pending' ? 14 : 0 }}>
                <span>By {author?.name ?? 'Unknown'}</span>
                <span>{timeAgo(idea.submittedAt)}</span>
                <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}><ThumbsUp size={12} /> {idea.votes}</span>
              </div>
              {idea.status === 'pending' && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
                  <button className="btn btn-sm" style={{ background: '#F0FDF4', color: 'var(--color-success)', border: '1px solid #BBF7D0', gap: 6 }} onClick={() => handleApprove(idea.id)}>
                    <CheckCircle size={13} /> Approve
                  </button>
                  <button className="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onClick={() => handleReject(idea.id)}>
                    <XCircle size={13} /> Reject
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/admin/AdminProjectsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { mockProjects } from '../../data/projects';
import { ProjectCard } from '../../components/ui/ProjectCard';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/StatusBadge';
import { projectStatusConfig } from '../../utils';
import { FilterBar } from '../../components/ui/FilterBar';
import { useState } from 'react';

const statusOpts = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'All' },
  { value: 'idea', label: 'Idea' },
  { value: 'in_progress', label: 'Active' },
  { value: 'review', label: 'Review' },
  { value: 'completed', label: 'Completed' },
  { value: 'incubated', label: 'Incubated' },
];

export function AdminProjectsPage() {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all');
  const filtered = filter === 'all' ? mockProjects : mockProjects.filter(p => p.status === filter);
  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Projects">
      <PageHeader title="Project Management" subtitle={`${mockProjects.length} total projects.`} />
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
        <FilterBar options={statusOpts} selected={filter} onChange={setFilter} />
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr))', gap: 16 }}>
        {filtered.map((p) => <ProjectCard key={p.id} project={p} />)}
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/faculty/FacultyDashboard.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { StatCard } from '../../components/ui/StatCard';
import { ProjectCard } from '../../components/ui/ProjectCard';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/StatusBadge';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { getProjectsByMentor } from '../../data/projects';
import { mockSessions, mockIdeas } from '../../data/mock';
import { ideaStatusConfig, formatDateTime } from '../../utils';
import { getUserById } from '../../data/users';
import { getInitials } from '../../utils';
import {
  FolderKanban, Calendar, Lightbulb, UserCheck, ArrowRight, Video
} from 'lucide-react';

export function FacultyDashboard() {
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const myProjects = getProjectsByMentor(currentUser.id);
  const mySessions = mockSessions.filter((s) => s.mentorId === currentUser.id);
  const pendingIdeas = mockIdeas.filter((i) => i.status === 'pending');

  const upcomingSession = mySessions.find((s) => s.status === 'scheduled');

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Faculty Dashboard">
      <PageHeader
        title={`Hello, ${currentUser.name.split(' ')[0]}! 👋`}
        subtitle={`${currentUser.department} · Faculty Mentor`}
      />

      {/* Stats */}
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(200px, 1fr))', gap: 16, marginBottom: 28 }}>
        <StatCard label="Projects Mentoring" value={myProjects.length} icon={<FolderKanban size={20} />} color="var(--color-accent)" />
        <StatCard label="Sessions" value={mySessions.length} icon={<Calendar size={20} />} color="var(--color-success)" description={`${mySessions.filter(s => s.status === 'scheduled').length} upcoming`} />
        <StatCard label="Pending Reviews" value={pendingIdeas.length} icon={<Lightbulb size={20} />} color="var(--color-warning)" />
        <StatCard label="Total Students" value={myProjects.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.memberIds.length, 0)} icon={<UserCheck size={20} />} color="var(--color-incubated)" />
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 340px', gap: 20, alignItems: 'start' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 20 }}>
          {/* Mentored Projects */}
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700 }}>Projects I'm Mentoring</h2>
              <Link to="/faculty/projects">
                <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style={{ gap: 4 }}>All <ArrowRight size={13} /></button>
              </Link>
            </div>
            {myProjects.length > 0 ? (
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
                {myProjects.map((p) => (
                  <ProjectCard key={p.id} project={p} compact href={`/faculty/projects/${p.id}`} />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>No projects assigned yet.</p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Pending Idea Reviews */}
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700 }}>Ideas Pending Review</h2>
              <Link to="/faculty/ideas">
                <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style={{ gap: 4 }}>Review <ArrowRight size={13} /></button>
              </Link>
            </div>
            {pendingIdeas.length > 0 ? (
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>
                {pendingIdeas.map((idea) => {
                  const author = getUserById(idea.submittedBy);
                  return (
                    <div key={idea.id} style={{ padding: '12px', background: 'var(--color-bg)', borderRadius: 8, border: '1px solid var(--color-border)' }}>
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 8, marginBottom: 6 }}>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{idea.title}</p>
                        <StatusBadge {...ideaStatusConfig['pending']} size="sm" />
                      </div>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                        By {author?.name} · {idea.votes} votes
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>No ideas pending review.</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Column */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
          {/* Next Session */}
          {upcomingSession && (
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 14 }}>Next Session</h2>
              <div style={{ background: 'var(--color-accent-light)', borderRadius: 10, padding: 14 }}>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 6 }}>
                  {upcomingSession.title}
                </p>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 10 }}>
                  📅 {formatDateTime(upcomingSession.scheduledAt)}<br />
                  ⏱ {upcomingSession.durationMinutes} minutes
                </p>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 }}>
                  {upcomingSession.studentIds.map((sid) => {
                    const u = getUserById(sid);
                    return u ? (
                      <div
                        key={sid}
                        title={u.name}
                        style={{
                          width: 28,
                          height: 28,
                          borderRadius: '50%',
                          background: 'var(--color-primary)',
                          color: '#fff',
                          display: 'flex',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          justifyContent: 'center',
                          fontSize: 10,
                          fontWeight: 700,
                        }}
                      >
                        {getInitials(u.name)}
                      </div>
                    ) : null;
                  })}
                </div>
                {upcomingSession.meetLink && (
                  <a href={upcomingSession.meetLink} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                    <button className="btn btn-primary btn-sm" style={{ gap: 6 }}>
                      <Video size={13} /> Join Meeting
                    </button>
                  </a>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* All Sessions */}
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 14 }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700 }}>Sessions</h2>
              <Link to="/faculty/sessions">
                <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">View all</button>
              </Link>
            </div>
            {mySessions.map((s) => (
              <div key={s.id} style={{ padding: '10px 0', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)' }}>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 3 }}>{s.title}</p>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center' }}>
                  <StatusBadge
                    label={s.status}
                    color={s.status === 'scheduled' ? 'var(--color-accent)' : s.status === 'completed' ? 'var(--color-success)' : 'var(--color-error)'}
                    bg={s.status === 'scheduled' ? 'var(--color-accent-light)' : s.status === 'completed' ? '#F0FDF4' : '#FEF2F2'}
                    size="sm"
                  />
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                    {formatDateTime(s.scheduledAt)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/faculty/FacultyIdeasPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { Modal } from '../../components/ui/Modal';
import { FormField } from '../../components/ui/FormField';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/StatusBadge';
import { useAppStore } from '../../hooks/useAppStore';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { useToast } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { getUserById } from '../../data/users';
import { ideaStatusConfig, formatDate } from '../../utils';
import { CheckCircle2, XCircle, Clock, RotateCcw, Eye, ThumbsUp, Filter } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Idea, IdeaStatus } from '../../types';

const DECISIONS: { value: IdeaStatus; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode; color: string; desc: string }[] = [
  { value: 'approved', label: 'Approve', icon: <CheckCircle2 size={16} />, color: '#22C55E', desc: 'This idea is feasible and aligned with club goals. The student may proceed to team formation.' },
  { value: 'pending', label: 'Request Changes', icon: <RotateCcw size={16} />, color: '#F59E0B', desc: 'The idea has potential but needs revision. Your feedback will be sent to the student.' },
  { value: 'rejected', label: 'Reject', icon: <XCircle size={16} />, color: '#EF4444', desc: 'This idea is not suitable in its current form. Provide constructive feedback.' },
];

export function FacultyIdeasPage() {
  const { ideas, updateIdeaStatus } = useAppStore();
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { addToast } = useToast();

  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Idea | null>(null);
  const [decision, setDecision] = useState<IdeaStatus>('approved');
  const [comment, setComment] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<IdeaStatus | 'all'>('all');

  const reviewableIdeas = ideas.filter((i) => i.status === 'pending' || i.status === 'approved' || i.status === 'rejected');
  const filtered = statusFilter === 'all' ? reviewableIdeas : reviewableIdeas.filter((i) => i.status === statusFilter);

  const pendingCount = ideas.filter((i) => i.status === 'pending').length;

  function handleSubmitDecision() {
    if (!selected) return;
    if (!comment.trim()) {
      addToast('error', 'Review comment required', 'Please add a review note for the student.');
      return;
    }
    setSubmitting(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      updateIdeaStatus(selected.id, decision, comment);
      setSubmitting(false);
      setSelected(null);
      setComment('');
      const decisionCfg = DECISIONS.find((d) => d.value === decision);
      addToast('success', `Idea ${decisionCfg?.label}d`, `Your decision has been recorded and the student will be notified.`);
    }, 800);
  }

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Idea Review Queue">
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 880 }}>
        {/* Header */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', marginBottom: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 12 }}>
          <div>
            <h1 style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 4 }}>Idea Review Queue</h1>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
              {pendingCount} idea{pendingCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} awaiting your review
            </p>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
            <Filter size={14} style={{ color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }} />
            <select className="input" style={{ width: 160 }} value={statusFilter} onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value as IdeaStatus | 'all')}>
              <option value="all">All Statuses</option>
              <option value="pending">Pending</option>
              <option value="approved">Approved</option>
              <option value="rejected">Rejected</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Summary Stats */}
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)', gap: 12, marginBottom: 24 }}>
          {[
            { label: 'Pending Review', value: ideas.filter((i) => i.status === 'pending').length, color: '#F59E0B', bg: '#FFFBEB' },
            { label: 'Approved', value: ideas.filter((i) => i.status === 'approved' || i.status === 'converted').length, color: '#22C55E', bg: '#F0FDF4' },
            { label: 'Rejected', value: ideas.filter((i) => i.status === 'rejected').length, color: '#EF4444', bg: '#FEF2F2' },
          ].map((stat) => (
            <div key={stat.label} className="card" style={{ padding: '16px 20px', borderLeft: `4px solid ${stat.color}` }}>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 800, color: stat.color }}>{stat.value}</p>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>{stat.label}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Ideas List */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
          {filtered.map((idea) => {
            const submitter = getUserById(idea.submittedBy);
            const cfg = ideaStatusConfig[idea.status];
            return (
              <div key={idea.id} className="card" style={{ padding: 20, display: 'flex', gap: 16, alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
                <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginBottom: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    <StatusBadge {...cfg} />
                    <span className="badge">{idea.category}</span>
                    <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                      <ThumbsUp size={11} /> {idea.votes} votes
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 6 }}>{idea.title}</h3>
                  <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.55, marginBottom: 10 }}>
                    {idea.description.slice(0, 160)}{idea.description.length > 160 ? '…' : ''}
                  </p>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, alignItems: 'center', fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                    <span>By <strong style={{ color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{submitter?.name ?? 'Unknown'}</strong></span>
                    <span>·</span>
                    <span>{formatDate(idea.submittedAt)}</span>
                    {idea.reviewNote && (
                      <>
                        <span>·</span>
                        <span style={{ color: '#F59E0B' }}>📝 Has review note</span>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button
                  className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
                  onClick={() => { setSelected(idea); setDecision('approved'); setComment(idea.reviewNote ?? ''); }}
                  style={{ gap: 6, flexShrink: 0 }}
                >
                  <Eye size={13} /> Review
                </button>
              </div>
            );
          })}
          {filtered.length === 0 && (
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
              No ideas match the current filter.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Review Modal */}
      <Modal open={!!selected} onClose={() => setSelected(null)} title="Review Idea" size="lg">
        {selected && (
          <>
            <div style={{ background: 'var(--color-bg)', borderRadius: 10, padding: 16, marginBottom: 20 }}>
              <h3 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 8 }}>{selected.title}</h3>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.65 }}>{selected.description}</p>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, marginTop: 10, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                <span>By: <strong>{getUserById(selected.submittedBy)?.name}</strong></span>
                <span>Category: <strong>{selected.category}</strong></span>
                <span>Votes: <strong>{selected.votes}</strong></span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Decision Buttons */}
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 10 }}>Your Decision</p>
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)', gap: 8 }}>
                {DECISIONS.map((d) => (
                  <button
                    key={d.value}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setDecision(d.value)}
                    style={{
                      padding: '10px 12px',
                      borderRadius: 10,
                      border: `2px solid ${decision === d.value ? d.color : 'var(--color-border)'}`,
                      background: decision === d.value ? `${d.color}18` : 'transparent',
                      cursor: 'pointer',
                      fontFamily: 'inherit',
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      gap: 6,
                      fontSize: 13,
                      fontWeight: 600,
                      color: decision === d.value ? d.color : 'var(--color-text)',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                      transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                    }}
                  >
                    <span style={{ color: d.color }}>{d.icon}</span>
                    {d.label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
              <div style={{ marginTop: 10, padding: '10px 14px', background: 'var(--color-bg)', borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                {DECISIONS.find((d) => d.value === decision)?.desc}
              </div>
            </div>

            <FormField label="Review Comment" required hint="Your feedback will be visible to the student.">
              <textarea
                className="input"
                rows={4}
                placeholder="Provide constructive feedback about this idea..."
                value={comment}
                onChange={(e) => setComment(e.target.value)}
                style={{ resize: 'vertical' }}
              />
            </FormField>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10, marginTop: 16 }}>
              <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => setSelected(null)}>Cancel</button>
              <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={handleSubmitDecision} disabled={submitting || !comment.trim()}>
                {submitting ? 'Submitting…' : 'Submit Decision'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </Modal>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}

export function FacultySessionsPage() {

            scheduled: { label: 'Scheduled', color: 'var(--color-accent)', bg: 'var(--color-accent-light)' },

              </p>

              {session.meetLink && session.status === 'scheduled' && (
                <a href={session.meetLink} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                  <button className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"><Video size={13} /> Join Meeting</button>
                </a>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

// Expanded article content keyed by slug
const articleContent: Record<string, string> = {
  'getting-started-with-llms': `
## Introduction

Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed the way we think about AI. As a college student, you don't need a PhD to start experimenting — you just need curiosity and a laptop.

## What Is an LLM?

An LLM is a neural network trained on massive amounts of text to predict and generate language. Models like GPT-4, Claude, and Gemini power everything from chatbots to code generators.

## Step 1: Understand the Basics

Before diving into code, grasp these core concepts:

- **Tokens**: LLMs process text as chunks called tokens, not characters or words
- **Context window**: The maximum amount of text a model can "see" at once
- **Temperature**: Controls creativity — higher values = more random outputs
- **Prompt engineering**: The art of crafting inputs to get better outputs

## Step 2: Set Up Your Environment

\`\`\`bash
pip install openai python-dotenv
\`\`\`

Create a \`.env\` file:
\`\`\`
OPENAI_API_KEY=your_key_here
\`\`\`

## Step 3: Your First API Call

\`\`\`python
import openai
from dotenv import load_dotenv
import os

load_dotenv()
client = openai.OpenAI()

response = client.chat.completions.create(
    model="gpt-4o-mini",
    messages=[
        {"role": "system", "content": "You are a helpful study assistant."},
        {"role": "user", "content": "Explain recursion in simple terms."}
    ]
)

print(response.choices[0].message.content)
\`\`\`

## Step 4: Build Something Useful

Try these beginner projects:

1. **Study Q&A bot** — answers questions from your notes
2. **Essay feedback tool** — critiques writing style and structure
3. **Code explainer** — paste code and get explanations in plain English

## Tips for College Students

- Use free tiers (OpenAI, Google Gemini, Groq) to stay within budget
- Explore open-source models like Llama 3 via Ollama for offline use
- Join the club's AI study group for hands-on sessions

## Conclusion

LLMs are no longer just for researchers. With just a few lines of Python, you can build powerful AI-powered tools. Start small, experiment often, and share what you learn with the club!
  `,
  'smart-attendance-system-case-study': `
## The Problem

Manual attendance was taking up to 10 minutes of every class, encouraging proxy marking, and leaving faculty with no reliable analytics. We wanted to fix this — for good.

## Our Approach

We started by talking to 12 faculty members across 4 departments. Their core needs: automation, reliability, and minimal hardware cost.

## Architecture

Our stack:
- **Raspberry Pi 4** as the edge compute unit per classroom
- **OpenCV** for face detection and recognition
- **RFID reader** as a backup verification method
- **FastAPI** backend with PostgreSQL
- **React** dashboard for faculty

## Month 1: Proof of Concept

We got face detection working in 3 days using pre-trained Haar cascades, then switched to a more accurate dlib model by week 2. The RFID reader was wired in by day 18.

## Month 2: Backend and Integration

The biggest challenge was the ERP integration. We had to reverse-engineer the college portal's API (with permission!) to push attendance data in real-time.

## Month 3: Testing and Deployment

We ran a 3-week pilot in Lab 301 with Dr. Meena Iyer's permission. Accuracy was 94.7% — better than we expected.

## Key Lessons

1. Talk to users before writing a single line of code
2. Perfect is the enemy of shipped — our v1 had no dashboard at all
3. Privacy matters — we stored face embeddings, never raw images
4. Hardware fails. Always have a backup plan (hence the RFID)

## Current Status

The system is now live in 3 classrooms. We're working on the faculty analytics dashboard. Check our GitHub for the full source code!

  `,
  'react-19-features': `
## React 19 is Here

After years of anticipation, React 19 brings some of the most significant changes to the library since hooks. Here's what you need to start using today.

## 1. The React Compiler

The biggest addition — an automatic memoization compiler. You can delete most of your \`useMemo\`, \`useCallback\`, and \`memo\` wrappers. The compiler handles it.

\`\`\`jsx
// React 18: manual optimization
const memoizedValue = useMemo(() => computeExpensive(a, b), [a, b]);

// React 19: just write normal code
const value = computeExpensive(a, b); // compiler handles it
\`\`\`

## 2. Actions

A new pattern for handling form submissions and async operations. Forms can now directly call async functions.

\`\`\`jsx
async function updateName(formData) {
  'use server';
  await db.users.update({ name: formData.get('name') });
}

<form action={updateName}>
  <input name="name" />
  <button type="submit">Update</button>
</form>
\`\`\`

## 3. New Hooks

- **useActionState** — manage state for form actions
- **useOptimistic** — show optimistic UI updates before server confirmation
- **useFormStatus** — access parent form's pending state

## 4. Improved Error Handling

React 19 adds better error boundaries and improved hydration error messages. No more cryptic "Text content did not match" errors without context.

## 5. Document Metadata

You can now render \`<title>\`, \`<meta>\`, and \`<link>\` tags anywhere in your tree and React will hoist them to \`<head>\`:

\`\`\`jsx
function BlogPost({ title }) {
  return (
    <article>
      <title>{title}</title>
      <meta name="description" content="..." />
      <h1>{title}</h1>
    </article>
  );
}
\`\`\`

## Getting Started

\`\`\`bash
npm install react@19 react-dom@19
\`\`\`

The ecosystem is rapidly catching up — most major libraries already support React 19.
  `,
};

export function ArticlePage() {
  const { slug } = useParams<{ slug: string }>();
  const post = mockBlogPosts.find((p) => p.slug === slug);

  if (!post) return <NotFoundPage />;

  const author = getUserById(post.authorId);
  const content = articleContent[post.slug] ?? post.content;
  const related = mockBlogPosts.filter((p) => p.id !== post.id && p.tags.some((t) => post.tags.includes(t))).slice(0, 2);

  // Simple markdown-ish renderer
  function renderContent(md: string) {
    const lines = md.trim().split('\n');
    const elements: React.ReactNode[] = [];
    let listItems: string[] = [];
    let codeLines: string[] = [];
    let inCode = false;
    let codeKey = 0;

    const flushList = (key: string) => {
      if (listItems.length > 0) {
        elements.push(
          <ul key={`list-${key}`} style={{ paddingLeft: 20, marginBottom: 16 }}>
            {listItems.map((li, i) => (
              <li key={i} style={{ fontSize: 15, color: 'var(--color-text)', lineHeight: 1.7, marginBottom: 4 }}
                dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: formatInline(li) }} />
            ))}
          </ul>
        );
        listItems = [];
      }
    };

    lines.forEach((line, i) => {
      if (line.startsWith('```')) {
        if (inCode) {
          elements.push(
            <pre key={`code-${codeKey++}`} style={{ background: 'var(--color-primary)', color: '#E2E8F0', padding: '16px 20px', borderRadius: 10, overflowX: 'auto', marginBottom: 20, fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
              <code>{codeLines.join('\n')}</code>
            </pre>
          );
          codeLines = [];
          inCode = false;
        } else {
          flushList(String(i));
          inCode = true;
        }
        return;
      }
      if (inCode) { codeLines.push(line); return; }
      if (line.startsWith('## ')) {
        flushList(String(i));
        elements.push(<h2 key={i} style={{ fontSize: 20, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 10, marginTop: 28 }}>{line.slice(3)}</h2>);
      } else if (line.startsWith('### ')) {
        flushList(String(i));
        elements.push(<h3 key={i} style={{ fontSize: 17, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 8, marginTop: 20 }}>{line.slice(4)}</h3>);
      } else if (line.startsWith('- ') || line.startsWith('* ')) {
        listItems.push(line.slice(2));
      } else if (line.trim() === '') {
        flushList(String(i));
      } else {
        flushList(String(i));
        elements.push(<p key={i} style={{ fontSize: 15, color: 'var(--color-text)', lineHeight: 1.8, marginBottom: 14 }} dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: formatInline(line) }} />);
      }
    });
    flushList('end');
    return elements;
  }

  function formatInline(text: string): string {
    return text
      .replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
      .replace(/`(.*?)`/g, '<code style="background:#F1F5F9;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-size:13px;color:#17365D">$1</code>')
      .replace(/\*(.*?)\*/g, '<em>$1</em>');
  }

  return (
    <PublicLayout>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 780, margin: '0 auto', padding: '32px 24px' }}>
        {/* Breadcrumb */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 24 }}>
          <Link to="/learn" style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)', textDecoration: 'none' }}>Learn</Link>
          <ChevronRight size={14} />
          <span style={{ overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>{post.title}</span>
        </div>

        {/* Header */}
        <div style={{ marginBottom: 32 }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap', marginBottom: 16 }}>
            <span className="badge" style={{ background: 'var(--color-accent-light)', color: 'var(--color-accent)' }}>{post.category}</span>
            {post.featured && <span className="badge" style={{ background: '#FFFBEB', color: '#F59E0B' }}>⭐ Featured</span>}
          </div>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: 30, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', lineHeight: 1.25, marginBottom: 16 }}>{post.title}</h1>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 16, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.65, marginBottom: 20 }}>{post.excerpt}</p>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 20, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
              <div style={{ width: 36, height: 36, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'var(--color-primary)', color: '#fff', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700 }}>
                {author ? getInitials(author.name) : '?'}
              </div>
              <div>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{author?.name ?? 'Unknown Author'}</p>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>{author?.department}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
              <span>{formatDate(post.publishedAt)}</span>
              <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}><Clock size={13} /> {post.readingTime} min read</span>
              <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}><Eye size={13} /> {post.views.toLocaleString()} views</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <hr style={{ borderColor: 'var(--color-border)', marginBottom: 32 }} />

        {/* Article Body */}
        <article style={{ marginBottom: 48 }}>
          {renderContent(content)}
        </article>

        {/* Tags */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap', marginBottom: 40 }}>
          <Tag size={14} style={{ color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginTop: 2 }} />
          {post.tags.map((tag) => (
            <span key={tag} className="badge" style={{ background: 'var(--color-bg)' }}>{tag}</span>
          ))}
        </div>

        <hr style={{ borderColor: 'var(--color-border)', marginBottom: 32 }} />

        {/* Related */}
        {related.length > 0 && (
          <div>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 16, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
              <BookOpen size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }} /> Related Articles
            </h2>
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr))', gap: 16 }}>
              {related.map((p) => <ArticleCard key={p.id} post={p} href={`/learn/${p.slug}`} />)}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div style={{ marginTop: 32 }}>
          <Link to="/learn">
            <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style={{ gap: 6 }}><ArrowLeft size={14} /> Back to Knowledge Hub</button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </PublicLayout>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { PublicLayout } from '../../layouts/PublicLayout';
import { Modal } from '../../components/ui/Modal';
import { FormField } from '../../components/ui/FormField';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/StatusBadge';
import { mockEvents } from '../../data/mock';
import { eventTypeConfig, formatDateTime, getDaysUntil, getInitials } from '../../utils';
import { useAppStore } from '../../hooks/useAppStore';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { useToast } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { NotFoundPage } from '../NotFoundPage';
import {
  Calendar, MapPin, Users, Clock, ChevronRight, Globe, Wifi,
  CheckCircle2, UserPlus
} from 'lucide-react';
import { getUserById } from '../../data/users';

const eventAgendas: Record<string, { time: string; title: string; speaker?: string }[]> = {
  e1: [
    { time: '9:00 AM', title: 'Inauguration & Team Registration' },
    { time: '10:00 AM', title: 'Problem Statement Reveal' },
    { time: '10:30 AM', title: 'Hacking Begins!' },
    { time: '1:00 PM', title: 'Lunch & Networking' },
    { time: '6:00 PM', title: 'Mid-point Check-in by Mentors' },

    { time: 'Day 2 12:00 PM', title: 'Project Demos & Judging' },
    { time: 'Day 2 3:00 PM', title: 'Closing Ceremony & Prize Distribution' },
  ],
  e2: [
    { time: '2:00 PM', title: 'Introduction to Blockchain & Solidity', speaker: 'Prof. Anil Kumar' },
    { time: '2:45 PM', title: 'Writing Your First Smart Contract' },

  ],

    { time: '10:00 AM', title: 'Welcome & GitHub Workflow Refresher' },
    { time: '10:30 AM', title: 'Open Contribution Sessions Begin' },
    { time: '1:00 PM', title: 'Lunch Break' },
    { time: '2:00 PM', title: 'Afternoon Contribution Sessions' },
    { time: '4:30 PM', title: 'Demo of Contributions + Wrap-up' },
  ],
};

export function EventDetailPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const event = mockEvents.find((e) => e.id === id);
  const { registeredEventIds, registerEvent, unregisterEvent } = useAppStore();
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { addToast } = useToast();

  const [regOpen, setRegOpen] = useState(false);
  const [regForm, setRegForm] = useState({ name: currentUser.name, email: currentUser.email, dietary: '' });
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  if (!event) return <NotFoundPage />;

  const cfg = eventTypeConfig[event.type];
  const daysUntil = getDaysUntil(event.startDate);
  const isPast = daysUntil < 0;
  const isRegistered = registeredEventIds.has(event.id);
  const spotsLeft = event.maxCapacity ? event.maxCapacity - event.registeredIds.length : null;
  const agenda = eventAgendas[event.id] ?? [];
  const organizer = getUserById(event.organizerId);

  function handleRegister() {
    setSubmitting(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      registerEvent(event!.id);
      setSubmitting(false);
      setRegOpen(false);
      addToast('success', 'Registered!', `You are now registered for ${event!.title}.`);
    }, 800);
  }

  function handleUnregister() {
    unregisterEvent(event!.id);
    addToast('info', 'Unregistered', `You have been removed from ${event!.title}.`);
  }

  return (
    <PublicLayout>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 1000, margin: '0 auto', padding: '32px 24px' }}>
        {/* Breadcrumb */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 24 }}>
          <Link to="/events" style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)', textDecoration: 'none' }}>Events</Link>
          <ChevronRight size={14} />
          <span style={{ overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>{event.title}</span>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 300px', gap: 24, alignItems: 'start' }}>
          {/* Left */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 20 }}>
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 28 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginBottom: 14, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                <StatusBadge label={cfg.label} color={cfg.color} bg={cfg.bg} />
                {isPast && <StatusBadge label="Past Event" color="#667085" bg="#F2F4F7" />}
                {!isPast && daysUntil <= 7 && <StatusBadge label={`${daysUntil}d to go`} color="#EF4444" bg="#FEF2F2" />}
                {event.isOnline && (
                  <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-accent)', background: 'var(--color-accent-light)', padding: '3px 10px', borderRadius: 99 }}>
                    <Wifi size={11} /> Online
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <h1 style={{ fontSize: 26, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 12 }}>{event.title}</h1>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 15, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.7 }}>{event.description}</p>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, marginTop: 14, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                {event.tags.map((t) => <span key={t} className="badge">{t}</span>)}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Agenda */}
            {agenda.length > 0 && (
              <div className="card" style={{ padding: 24 }}>
                <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 16, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                  <Calendar size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }} /> Agenda
                </h2>
                {agenda.map((item, i) => (
                  <div key={i} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, padding: '10px 0', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
                    <div style={{ minWidth: 90, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-accent)', paddingTop: 2 }}>{item.time}</div>
                    <div>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{item.title}</p>
                      {item.speaker && <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginTop: 2 }}>👤 {item.speaker}</p>}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Right Sidebar */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
            {/* Registration CTA */}
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
              {isRegistered ? (
                <>
                  <div style={{ background: '#F0FDF4', border: '1px solid #BBF7D0', borderRadius: 10, padding: '12px 16px', marginBottom: 14, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
                    <CheckCircle2 size={18} style={{ color: '#22C55E' }} />
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: '#15803D' }}>You're registered!</p>
                  </div>
                  {!isPast && (
                    <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style={{ width: '100%', color: 'var(--color-error)' }} onClick={handleUnregister}>
                      Cancel Registration
                    </button>
                  )}
                </>
              ) : isPast ? (
                <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', textAlign: 'center' }}>This event has ended.</p>
              ) : (
                <>
                  {spotsLeft !== null && spotsLeft < 20 && spotsLeft > 0 && (
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#EF4444', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 10 }}>⚠️ Only {spotsLeft} spots remaining!</p>
                  )}
                  <button className="btn btn-primary" style={{ width: '100%', gap: 8 }} onClick={() => setRegOpen(true)}>
                    <UserPlus size={15} /> Register Now
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Event Details */}
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
              <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 14 }}>Event Details</h3>
              {[
                { icon: <Calendar size={15} />, label: formatDateTime(event.startDate) },
                { icon: <Clock size={15} />, label: `Until ${formatDateTime(event.endDate)}` },
                { icon: event.isOnline ? <Globe size={15} /> : <MapPin size={15} />, label: event.location },
                { icon: <Users size={15} />, label: `${event.registeredIds.length}${event.maxCapacity ? `/${event.maxCapacity}` : '+'} registered` },
              ].map(({ icon, label }, i) => (
                <div key={i} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: '8px 0', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)', fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>
                  <span style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)', flexShrink: 0 }}>{icon}</span>
                  {label}
                </div>
              ))}
              {event.registrationDeadline && (
                <div style={{ marginTop: 12, padding: '10px 14px', background: '#FFFBEB', borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 13, color: '#92400E' }}>
                  📅 Registration closes: <strong>{formatDateTime(event.registrationDeadline)}</strong>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Organizer */}
            {organizer && (
              <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
                <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 12 }}>Organized by</h3>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
                  <div style={{ width: 36, height: 36, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'var(--color-primary)', color: '#fff', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700 }}>
                    {getInitials(organizer.name)}
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{organizer.name}</p>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>{organizer.department}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Registration Modal */}
      <Modal open={regOpen} onClose={() => setRegOpen(false)} title={`Register for ${event.title}`} size="sm">
        <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 20 }}>
          Confirm your details to register for this event. You'll receive a reminder notification.
        </p>
        <FormField label="Full Name">
          <input className="input" value={regForm.name} onChange={(e) => setRegForm((f) => ({ ...f, name: e.target.value }))} />
        </FormField>
        <FormField label="Email">
          <input className="input" value={regForm.email} onChange={(e) => setRegForm((f) => ({ ...f, email: e.target.value }))} type="email" />
        </FormField>
        {!event.isOnline && (
          <FormField label="Dietary preference (optional)">
            <input className="input" value={regForm.dietary} onChange={(e) => setRegForm((f) => ({ ...f, dietary: e.target.value }))} placeholder="e.g. Vegetarian, No restrictions" />
          </FormField>
        )}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 20, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
          <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => setRegOpen(false)}>Cancel</button>
          <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={handleRegister} disabled={submitting}>
            {submitting ? 'Registering…' : 'Confirm Registration'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </PublicLayout>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/public/EventsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { PublicLayout } from '../../layouts/PublicLayout';
import { EventCard } from '../../components/ui/EventCard';
import { SearchInput } from '../../components/ui/SearchInput';
import { FilterBar } from '../../components/ui/FilterBar';
import { EmptyState } from '../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { mockEvents } from '../../data/mock';
import { Calendar } from 'lucide-react';

const typeOptions = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'All Events' },
  { value: 'hackathon', label: 'Hackathons' },
  { value: 'workshop', label: 'Workshops' },
  { value: 'talk', label: 'Talks' },
  { value: 'meetup', label: 'Meetups' },
];

const modeOptions = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'All Modes' },
  { value: 'online', label: 'Online' },
  { value: 'offline', label: 'In-person' },
];

export function EventsPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState('all');
  const [modeFilter, setModeFilter] = useState('all');

  const filtered = mockEvents.filter((e) => {
    const matchSearch =
      !search ||
      e.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      e.description.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
    const matchType = typeFilter === 'all' || e.type === typeFilter;
    const matchMode =
      modeFilter === 'all' ||
      (modeFilter === 'online' && e.isOnline) ||
      (modeFilter === 'offline' && !e.isOnline);
    return matchSearch && matchType && matchMode;
  });

  return (
    <PublicLayout>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 1200, margin: '0 auto', padding: '40px 24px' }}>
        <PageHeader
          title="Events"
          subtitle="Hackathons, workshops, talks, and meetups — stay in the loop."
        />

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, marginBottom: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          <SearchInput
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search events…"
            style={{ width: 260 }}
          />
          <FilterBar options={typeOptions} selected={typeFilter} onChange={setTypeFilter} />
          <FilterBar options={modeOptions} selected={modeFilter} onChange={setModeFilter} />
        </div>

        {filtered.length > 0 ? (
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr))', gap: 16 }}>
            {filtered.map((event) => (
              <EventCard key={event.id} event={event} href={`/events/${event.id}`} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <EmptyState
            icon={<Calendar size={24} />}
            title="No events found"
            description="Check back soon or adjust your filters."
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </PublicLayout>
  );
}

  return (

        <div style={{ maxWidth: 820, margin: '0 auto', textAlign: 'center' }}>
          <div

              gap: 8,

        </div>
      </section>
    </PublicLayout>
  );
}

function GhostButton({ children, rightIcon, ...props }: React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & { rightIcon?: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <button className="btn btn-ghost" {...props} style={{ fontSize: 14, ...props.style }}>
      {children}
      {rightIcon}
    </button>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/public/IdeasPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { PublicLayout } from '../../layouts/PublicLayout';
import { FilterBar } from '../../components/ui/FilterBar';
import { EmptyState } from '../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/StatusBadge';
import { ideaStatusConfig, timeAgo, getInitials } from '../../utils';
import { getUserById } from '../../data/users';
import { ThumbsUp, Lightbulb, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { useToast } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { useAppStore } from '../../hooks/useAppStore';

const statusOptions = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'All' },
  { value: 'pending', label: 'Pending' },
  { value: 'approved', label: 'Approved' },
  { value: 'converted', label: 'Converted' },
];

export function IdeasPage() {
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('all');
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { addToast } = useToast();
  const { ideas, voteIdea } = useAppStore();

  const filtered = ideas.filter(
    (i) => statusFilter === 'all' || i.status === statusFilter
  );

  const sortedFiltered = [...filtered].sort((a, b) => b.votes - a.votes);

  function handleVote(id: string) {
    voteIdea(id, currentUser.id);
    const idea = ideas.find((i) => i.id === id);
    const hasVoted = idea?.votedBy.includes(currentUser.id);
    addToast(hasVoted ? 'info' : 'success', hasVoted ? 'Vote removed' : 'Upvoted!', hasVoted ? '' : 'Your vote has been recorded.');
  }

  return (
    <PublicLayout>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 900, margin: '0 auto', padding: '40px 24px' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 12, marginBottom: 20 }}>
          <PageHeader
            title="Ideas Board"
            subtitle="Browse community ideas, upvote your favourites, and see which ones become real projects."
          />
          <Link to="/ideas/submit">
            <button className="btn btn-primary" style={{ gap: 6 }}>
              <Plus size={15} /> Submit an Idea
            </button>
          </Link>
        </div>

        <FilterBar options={statusOptions} selected={statusFilter} onChange={setStatusFilter} />

        <div style={{ marginTop: 24, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
          {sortedFiltered.length > 0 ? (
            sortedFiltered.map((idea) => {
              const author = getUserById(idea.submittedBy);
              const cfg = ideaStatusConfig[idea.status];

              return (
                <div key={idea.id} className="card" style={{ padding: 20, display: 'flex', gap: 16 }}>
                  {/* Vote */}
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleVote(idea.id)}
                      aria-label={idea.votedBy.includes(currentUser.id) ? 'Remove vote' : 'Upvote idea'}
                      style={{
                        background: idea.votedBy.includes(currentUser.id) ? 'var(--color-accent-light)' : 'var(--color-bg)',
                        border: `1px solid ${idea.votedBy.includes(currentUser.id) ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-border)'}`,
                        borderRadius: 8,
                        padding: '8px 10px',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        display: 'flex',
                        flexDirection: 'column',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: 2,
                        color: idea.votedBy.includes(currentUser.id) ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
                        transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                        minWidth: 44,
                      }}
                    >
                      <ThumbsUp size={16} />
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700 }}>{idea.votes}</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  {/* Content */}
                  <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap', marginBottom: 6 }}>
                      <h3 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>
                        {idea.title}
                      </h3>
                      <StatusBadge {...cfg} size="sm" />
                    </div>

                    <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.6, marginBottom: 10 }}>
                      {idea.description}
                    </p>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 16, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                      <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                        <div
                          style={{
                            width: 20,
                            height: 20,
                            borderRadius: '50%',
                            background: 'var(--color-primary)',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            fontSize: 9,
                            fontWeight: 700,
                            color: '#fff',
                          }}
                        >
                          {author ? getInitials(author.name) : '?'}
                        </div>
                        {author?.name ?? 'Unknown'}
                      </span>
                      <span>{timeAgo(idea.submittedAt)}</span>
                      <span
                        style={{
                          background: 'var(--color-bg)',
                          border: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                          padding: '1px 8px',
                          borderRadius: 4,
                          fontSize: 11,
                        }}
                      >
                        {idea.category}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {idea.reviewNote && (
                      <div
                        style={{
                          marginTop: 10,
                          padding: '8px 12px',
                          background: idea.status === 'rejected' ? '#FEF2F2' : 'var(--color-accent-light)',
                          borderRadius: 6,
                          fontSize: 13,
                          color: idea.status === 'rejected' ? 'var(--color-error)' : 'var(--color-accent)',
                          borderLeft: `3px solid ${idea.status === 'rejected' ? 'var(--color-error)' : 'var(--color-accent)'}`,
                        }}
                      >
                        <strong>Reviewer note:</strong> {idea.reviewNote}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })
          ) : (
            <EmptyState
              icon={<Lightbulb size={24} />}
              title="No ideas found"
              description="Be the first to submit an idea!"
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </PublicLayout>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/public/IdeaSubmitPage.tsx
