import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { PublicLayout } from '../../layouts/PublicLayout';
import { FormField } from '../../components/ui/FormField';
import { useAppStore } from '../../hooks/useAppStore';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { useToast } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import type { Idea, ProjectCategory } from '../../types';
import { CheckCircle2, ChevronRight, ChevronLeft, Save, Send, Lightbulb } from 'lucide-react';

const CATEGORIES: ProjectCategory[] = [
  'Web Development', 'Mobile App', 'AI / ML', 'IoT', 'Cybersecurity',
  'Data Science', 'Blockchain', 'Game Dev', 'Open Source', 'Other',
];

const STEPS = ['Basic Info', 'Problem & Solution', 'Team & Skills', 'Review & Submit'];

interface IdeaFormData {
  title: string;
  category: ProjectCategory;
  problemStatement: string;
  proposedSolution: string;
  targetUsers: string;
  expectedBenefit: string;
  skillsRequired: string;
  teamPreference: 'individual' | 'team';
}

const EMPTY: IdeaFormData = {
  title: '',
  category: 'Web Development',
  problemStatement: '',
  proposedSolution: '',
  targetUsers: '',
  expectedBenefit: '',
  skillsRequired: '',
  teamPreference: 'team',
};

function Step1({ form, set }: { form: IdeaFormData; set: (f: IdeaFormData) => void }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
      <FormField label="Idea Title" required>
        <input
          className="input"
          placeholder="e.g. Smart Campus Parking System"
          value={form.title}
          onChange={(e) => set({ ...form, title: e.target.value })}
          maxLength={80}
        />
        <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginTop: 4 }}>{form.title.length}/80 characters</p>
      </FormField>
      <FormField label="Category" required>
        <select
          className="input"
          value={form.category}
          onChange={(e) => set({ ...form, category: e.target.value as ProjectCategory })}
        >
          {CATEGORIES.map((c) => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
        </select>
      </FormField>
    </div>
  );
}

function Step2({ form, set }: { form: IdeaFormData; set: (f: IdeaFormData) => void }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
      <FormField label="Problem Statement" required hint="What specific problem does your idea address? Be as specific as possible.">
        <textarea
          className="input"
          rows={4}
          placeholder="Describe the problem clearly. Who faces it? How often? What are its consequences?"
          value={form.problemStatement}
          onChange={(e) => set({ ...form, problemStatement: e.target.value })}
          style={{ resize: 'vertical' }}
        />
      </FormField>
      <FormField label="Proposed Solution" required hint="How does your idea solve the problem?">
        <textarea
          className="input"
          rows={4}
          placeholder="Describe your proposed solution. What will it do? How will it work?"
          value={form.proposedSolution}
          onChange={(e) => set({ ...form, proposedSolution: e.target.value })}
          style={{ resize: 'vertical' }}
        />
      </FormField>
      <FormField label="Target Users" required>
        <input
          className="input"
          placeholder="e.g. College students, Faculty members, Campus administration"
          value={form.targetUsers}
          onChange={(e) => set({ ...form, targetUsers: e.target.value })}
        />
      </FormField>
      <FormField label="Expected Benefit">
        <textarea
          className="input"
          rows={3}
          placeholder="What positive impact will this have? Quantify if possible."
          value={form.expectedBenefit}
          onChange={(e) => set({ ...form, expectedBenefit: e.target.value })}
          style={{ resize: 'vertical' }}
        />
      </FormField>
    </div>
  );
}

function Step3({ form, set }: { form: IdeaFormData; set: (f: IdeaFormData) => void }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
      <FormField label="Skills Required" hint="What technical or domain skills would be needed to build this?">
        <input
          className="input"
          placeholder="e.g. React, Python, Machine Learning, UI Design"
          value={form.skillsRequired}
          onChange={(e) => set({ ...form, skillsRequired: e.target.value })}
        />
      </FormField>
      <FormField label="Team Preference" required>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12 }}>
          {(['individual', 'team'] as const).map((opt) => (
            <button
              key={opt}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => set({ ...form, teamPreference: opt })}
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                padding: '12px 20px',
                borderRadius: 10,
                border: `2px solid ${form.teamPreference === opt ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-border)'}`,
                background: form.teamPreference === opt ? 'var(--color-accent-light)' : 'var(--color-surface)',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                fontFamily: 'inherit',
                fontSize: 14,
                fontWeight: form.teamPreference === opt ? 600 : 400,
                color: form.teamPreference === opt ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-text)',
                transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
              }}
            >
              {opt === 'individual' ? '👤 Individual' : '👥 Team (2-5 members)'}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </FormField>
    </div>
  );
}

function ReviewStep({ form }: { form: IdeaFormData }) {
  const fields = [
    { label: 'Title', value: form.title },
    { label: 'Category', value: form.category },
    { label: 'Problem Statement', value: form.problemStatement },
    { label: 'Proposed Solution', value: form.proposedSolution },
    { label: 'Target Users', value: form.targetUsers },
    { label: 'Expected Benefit', value: form.expectedBenefit || '—' },
    { label: 'Skills Required', value: form.skillsRequired || '—' },
    { label: 'Team Preference', value: form.teamPreference === 'individual' ? 'Individual' : 'Team (2-5 members)' },
  ];
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 0 }}>
      {fields.map(({ label, value }) => (
        <div key={label} style={{ padding: '12px 0', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)' }}>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 4, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>{label}</p>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text)', lineHeight: 1.6, whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap' }}>{value}</p>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function IdeaSubmitPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { submitIdea } = useAppStore();
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { addToast } = useToast();

  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [form, setForm] = useState<IdeaFormData>(EMPTY);
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  function validateStep(): string | null {
    if (step === 0) {
      if (!form.title.trim()) return 'Please enter an idea title.';
    }
    if (step === 1) {
      if (!form.problemStatement.trim()) return 'Please describe the problem.';
      if (!form.proposedSolution.trim()) return 'Please describe your solution.';
      if (!form.targetUsers.trim()) return 'Please specify target users.';
    }
    return null;
  }

  function handleNext() {
    const err = validateStep();
    if (err) { addToast('error', 'Validation Error', err); return; }
    setStep((s) => s + 1);
  }

  function handleSaveDraft() {
    addToast('info', 'Draft Saved', 'Your idea has been saved as a draft locally.');
    try { localStorage.setItem('itclub_idea_draft', JSON.stringify(form)); } catch {}
  }

  function handleSubmit() {
    setSubmitting(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      const idea: Idea = {
        id: `i-${Date.now()}`,
        title: form.title,
        description: `${form.problemStatement}\n\nSolution: ${form.proposedSolution}`,
        category: form.category,
        submittedBy: currentUser.id,
        submittedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        status: 'pending',
        votes: 0,
        votedBy: [],
      };
      submitIdea(idea);
      setSubmitting(false);
      setSubmitted(true);
      addToast('success', 'Idea Submitted!', 'Your idea is now in the faculty review queue.');
      try { localStorage.removeItem('itclub_idea_draft'); } catch {}
    }, 1000);
  }

  if (submitted) {
    return (
      <PublicLayout>
        <div style={{ maxWidth: 600, margin: '60px auto', padding: '0 24px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 72, marginBottom: 20 }}>🎉</div>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 12 }}>Idea Submitted!</h1>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 16, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.6, marginBottom: 32 }}>
            <strong>"{form.title}"</strong> has been submitted for faculty review. You'll receive a notification once it's reviewed. In the meantime, you can vote on other ideas!
          </p>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, justifyContent: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => navigate('/student/ideas')}>View My Ideas</button>
            <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => navigate('/ideas')}>Browse All Ideas</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </PublicLayout>
    );
  }

  return (
    <PublicLayout>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 680, margin: '0 auto', padding: '32px 24px' }}>
        {/* Header */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, marginBottom: 32 }}>
          <div style={{ width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 12, background: 'var(--color-accent-light)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', color: 'var(--color-accent)' }}>
            <Lightbulb size={22} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>Submit an Idea</h1>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>Share your innovation with the club — great ideas become real projects.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Stepper */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 28 }}>
          {STEPS.map((label, i) => (
            <React.Fragment key={label}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', flex: i < STEPS.length - 1 ? 'none' : 1 }}>
                <div style={{
                  width: 32, height: 32, borderRadius: '50%',
                  background: i < step ? 'var(--color-success)' : i === step ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-border)',
                  color: i <= step ? '#fff' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
                  display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                  fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
                  transition: 'all 0.2s ease',
                }}>
                  {i < step ? <CheckCircle2 size={16} /> : i + 1}
                </div>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: i === step ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginTop: 4, textAlign: 'center', fontWeight: i === step ? 600 : 400 }}>
                  {label}
                </span>
              </div>
              {i < STEPS.length - 1 && (
                <div style={{ flex: 1, height: 2, background: i < step ? 'var(--color-success)' : 'var(--color-border)', margin: '0 4px', marginBottom: 20, transition: 'background 0.3s ease' }} />
              )}
            </React.Fragment>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Form Card */}
        <div className="card" style={{ padding: 28 }}>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 20 }}>{STEPS[step]}</h2>
          {step === 0 && <Step1 form={form} set={setForm} />}
          {step === 1 && <Step2 form={form} set={setForm} />}
          {step === 2 && <Step3 form={form} set={setForm} />}
          {step === 3 && <ReviewStep form={form} />}

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 10 }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
              <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onClick={() => setStep((s) => s - 1)} disabled={step === 0} style={{ gap: 6 }}>
                <ChevronLeft size={14} /> Previous
              </button>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
              <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onClick={handleSaveDraft} style={{ gap: 6 }}>
                <Save size={14} /> Save Draft
              </button>
              {step < STEPS.length - 1 ? (
                <button className="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onClick={handleNext} style={{ gap: 6 }}>
                  Next <ChevronRight size={14} />
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button className="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onClick={handleSubmit} disabled={submitting} style={{ gap: 6 }}>
                  <Send size={14} /> {submitting ? 'Submitting…' : 'Submit for Review'}
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </PublicLayout>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/public/LearnPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { PublicLayout } from '../../layouts/PublicLayout';
import { ArticleCard } from '../../components/ui/ArticleCard';
import { SearchInput } from '../../components/ui/SearchInput';
import { FilterBar } from '../../components/ui/FilterBar';
import { EmptyState } from '../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { mockBlogPosts, mockQuizzes } from '../../data/mock';
import { BookOpen, Clock, Zap } from 'lucide-react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

const difficultyColors = {
  beginner: { color: '#22C55E', bg: '#F0FDF4' },
  intermediate: { color: '#F59E0B', bg: '#FFFBEB' },
  advanced: { color: '#EF4444', bg: '#FEF2F2' },
};

export function LearnPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'articles' | 'quizzes'>('articles');

  const filteredArticles = mockBlogPosts.filter(
    (p) =>
      !search ||
      p.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.tags.some((t) => t.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()))
  );

  const filteredQuizzes = mockQuizzes.filter(
    (q) =>
      !search ||
      q.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      q.category.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <PublicLayout>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 1200, margin: '0 auto', padding: '40px 24px' }}>
        <PageHeader
          title="Learn"
          subtitle="Articles, tutorials, and quizzes curated by club members and faculty."
        />

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, marginBottom: 24, borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)', paddingBottom: 0 }}>
          {(['articles', 'quizzes'] as const).map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t}
              onClick={() => setTab(t)}
              style={{
                padding: '8px 20px',
                fontSize: 14,
                fontWeight: tab === t ? 600 : 400,
                color: tab === t ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)',
                background: 'none',
                border: 'none',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                borderBottom: tab === t ? '2px solid var(--color-accent)' : '2px solid transparent',
                marginBottom: -1,
                fontFamily: 'inherit',
                transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
              }}
            >
              {t.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + t.slice(1)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <SearchInput
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={tab === 'articles' ? 'Search articles…' : 'Search quizzes…'}
          style={{ width: 300, marginBottom: 24 }}
        />

        {tab === 'articles' && (
          filteredArticles.length > 0 ? (
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr))', gap: 16 }}>
              {filteredArticles.map((post) => (
                <ArticleCard key={post.id} post={post} href={`/learn/${post.slug}`} />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <EmptyState icon={<BookOpen size={24} />} title="No articles found" description="Try a different search term." />
          )
        )}

        {tab === 'quizzes' && (
          filteredQuizzes.length > 0 ? (
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr))', gap: 16 }}>
              {filteredQuizzes.map((quiz) => {
                const diff = difficultyColors[quiz.difficulty];
                return (
                  <div key={quiz.id} className="card card-hover" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 }}>
                      <span className="badge" style={{ color: diff.color, background: diff.bg }}>
                        {quiz.difficulty}
                      </span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                        {quiz.category}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 8, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>
                      {quiz.title}
                    </h3>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 16, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
                      {quiz.description}
                    </p>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                        <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
                          <Zap size={12} /> {quiz.questions.length} questions
                        </span>
                        <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
                          <Clock size={12} /> {quiz.estimatedMinutes} min
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <Link to={`/learn/quiz/${quiz.id}`}>
                        <button className="btn btn-primary btn-sm">Start Quiz</button>
                      </Link>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <EmptyState icon={<Zap size={24} />} title="No quizzes found" description="Try a different search term." />
          )
        )}
      </div>
    </PublicLayout>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useParams, Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';

  UserPlus, ChevronRight, Briefcase, Target, Layers, Code

  { id: 'm3', label: 'Hardware Prototype v1', done: true, date: '2025-12-01' },
  { id: 'm4', label: 'Backend API Integration', done: true, date: '2026-02-15' },
  { id: 'm5', label: 'Dashboard UI', done: false, date: '2026-11-10' },
  { id: 'm6', label: 'Testing & QA', done: false, date: '2026-12-01' },
  { id: 'm7', label: 'Deployment & Handover', done: false, date: '2027-01-15' },
];

const techStack: Record<string, string[]> = {
  p1: ['Python', 'OpenCV', 'Raspberry Pi', 'RFID', 'React', 'FastAPI', 'PostgreSQL'],
  p2: ['React', 'FastAPI', 'LangChain', 'OpenAI API', 'PostgreSQL', 'Redis'],
  p3: ['React Native', 'Expo', 'Firebase', 'TypeScript', 'Node.js'],
  p4: ['Rust', 'WebAssembly', 'React', 'TypeScript', 'SQLite'],
  p5: ['Arduino', 'LoRa', 'Python', 'InfluxDB', 'Grafana'],
  p6: ['React', 'Node.js', 'PostgreSQL', 'PWA', 'IndexedDB'],
};

export function ProjectDetailPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const project = getProjectById(id ?? '');
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { submitApplication, applications } = useAppStore();
  const { addToast } = useToast();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [joinOpen, setJoinOpen] = useState(false);
  const [joinForm, setJoinForm] = useState({ message: '', skills: '' });
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  if (!project) return <NotFoundPage />;

  const lead = getUserById(project!.leadId);
  const mentor = project!.mentorId ? getUserById(project!.mentorId) : undefined;
  const members = project!.memberIds.map((m) => getUserById(m)).filter(Boolean);
  const stack = techStack[project!.id] ?? project!.tags;
  const cfg = projectStatusConfig[project!.status];

  const alreadyApplied = applications.some(
    (a) => a.projectId === project.id && a.applicantId === currentUser.id
  );
  const isMember = project.memberIds.includes(currentUser.id);

  function handleJoin() {
    if (!joinForm.message.trim()) {
      addToast('error', 'Please add a message', 'Tell the team why you want to join.');
      return;
    }
    const pid = project!.id;
    setSubmitting(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      const app: ProjectApplication = {
        id: `app-${Date.now()}`,
        projectId: pid,
        applicantId: currentUser.id,
        message: joinForm.message,
        skills: joinForm.skills.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean),
        appliedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        status: 'pending',
      };
      submitApplication(app);
      setSubmitting(false);
      setJoinOpen(false);
      addToast('success', 'Application Submitted!', 'The project lead will review your request shortly.');
      setJoinForm({ message: '', skills: '' });
    }, 800);
  }

  const completedMilestones = milestones.filter((m) => m.done).length;
  const progress = Math.round((completedMilestones / milestones.length) * 100);

  return (
    <PublicLayout>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 1100, margin: '0 auto', padding: '32px 24px' }}>
        {/* Breadcrumb */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 20 }}>
          <Link to="/projects" style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)', textDecoration: 'none' }}>Projects</Link>
          <ChevronRight size={14} />
          <span>{project.title}</span>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 300px', gap: 24, alignItems: 'start' }}>
          {/* Left */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 20 }}>
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 28 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap', marginBottom: 12 }}>
                <StatusBadge {...cfg} />
                <span className="badge" style={{ background: 'var(--color-bg)', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', border: '1px solid var(--color-border)' }}>
                  {project.category}
                </span>
              </div>
              <h1 style={{ fontSize: 26, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 12 }}>{project.title}</h1>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 15, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.7, marginBottom: 16 }}>
                {project.longDescription ?? project.description}
              </p>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                {project.tags.map((tag) => (
                  <span key={tag} className="badge">{tag}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
              {(project!.githubUrl || project!.demoUrl) && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 16 }}>
                  {project!.githubUrl && (
                    <a href={project!.githubUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                      <button className="btn btn-sm btn-ghost" style={{ gap: 6 }}><Code size={14} /> GitHub</button>
                    </a>
                  )}
                  {project!.demoUrl && (
                    <a href={project!.demoUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                      <button className="btn btn-sm btn-ghost" style={{ gap: 6 }}><ExternalLink size={14} /> Live Demo</button>
                    </a>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Problem & Solution */}
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 24 }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 16, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                <Target size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }} /> Problem & Solution
              </h2>
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 16 }}>
                <div style={{ background: '#FEF2F2', borderRadius: 10, padding: 16, borderLeft: '3px solid #EF4444' }}>
                  <p style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: '#EF4444', marginBottom: 8, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>Problem</p>
                  <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text)', lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
                    {project.id === 'p1' && 'Manual attendance is unreliable, time-consuming, and prone to proxy marking. Faculty spend up to 10 minutes per class on roll calls.'}
                    {project.id === 'p2' && 'Students struggle with one-size-fits-all study materials that don\'t adapt to their learning pace, gaps, or style.'}
                    {project.id === 'p3' && 'Campus information is scattered across WhatsApp groups, notice boards, and emails with no unified discovery channel.'}
                    {project.id === 'p4' && 'Students reuse weak passwords across accounts due to the complexity of managing secure credentials.'}
                    {project.id === 'p5' && 'Small-scale farmers lack affordable tools to monitor soil health, leading to overuse of water and fertilizers.'}
                    {project.id === 'p6' && 'Existing LMS platforms are expensive, internet-dependent, and lack support for Indian regional languages.'}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div style={{ background: '#F0FDF4', borderRadius: 10, padding: 16, borderLeft: '3px solid #22C55E' }}>
                  <p style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: '#22C55E', marginBottom: 8, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>Solution</p>
                  <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text)', lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
                    {project.id === 'p1' && 'IoT-based system using facial recognition + RFID for seamless, automated attendance with real-time ERP integration and parent alerts.'}
                    {project.id === 'p2' && 'LLM-powered conversational study assistant that generates personalized quizzes, explains concepts, and adapts to the student\'s curriculum.'}
                    {project.id === 'p3' && 'A unified mobile platform for club discovery, event registration, peer networking, and skill exchange marketplace.'}
                    {project.id === 'p4' && 'Open-source, end-to-end encrypted password manager with zero-knowledge architecture — your data is always yours.'}
                    {project.id === 'p5' && 'Low-cost LoRa sensor networks providing real-time soil analytics with actionable recommendations, all accessible via SMS.'}
                    {project.id === 'p6' && 'Open-source, offline-first LMS with PWA support, regional language UI, and lightweight enough for basic smartphones.'}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Tech Stack */}
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 24 }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 16, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                <Layers size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }} /> Technology Stack
              </h2>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                {stack.map((tech) => (
                  <span
                    key={tech}
                    style={{
                      padding: '6px 14px',
                      background: 'var(--color-primary)',
                      color: '#fff',
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      fontSize: 13,
                      fontWeight: 500,
                    }}
                  >
                    {tech}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Milestones */}
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 24 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }}>
                <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                  <Briefcase size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }} /> Milestones
                </h2>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-accent)' }}>{progress}% complete</span>
              </div>
              <div style={{ height: 6, background: 'var(--color-border)', borderRadius: 99, marginBottom: 20, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
                <div style={{ height: '100%', width: `${progress}%`, background: 'var(--color-accent)', borderRadius: 99, transition: 'width 0.5s ease' }} />
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>
                {milestones.map((m) => (
                  <div key={m.id} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, padding: '8px 0', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)' }}>
                    {m.done
                      ? <CheckCircle2 size={18} style={{ color: '#22C55E', flexShrink: 0 }} />
                      : <Circle size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--color-border)', flexShrink: 0 }} />}
                    <span style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: 14, color: m.done ? 'var(--color-text)' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)', textDecoration: m.done ? 'none' : 'none', fontWeight: m.done ? 500 : 400 }}>
                      {m.label}
                    </span>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
                      <Clock size={11} /> {formatDate(m.date)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right Sidebar */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
            {/* CTA */}
            {project.isOpenForApplications && !isMember && (
              <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20, background: 'var(--color-accent-light)', border: '1.5px solid var(--color-accent)' }}>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-accent)', marginBottom: 6 }}>
                  🔓 Open for Applications
                </p>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 14 }}>
                  {project.applicationCount} application{project.applicationCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} received so far.
                </p>
                {alreadyApplied ? (
                  <div style={{ padding: '10px 14px', background: '#F0FDF4', border: '1px solid #BBF7D0', borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 13, color: '#15803D', fontWeight: 500 }}>
                    ✅ You have already applied
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <button className="btn btn-primary" style={{ width: '100%', gap: 8 }} onClick={() => setJoinOpen(true)}>
                    <UserPlus size={15} /> Request to Join
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
            {isMember && (
              <div className="card" style={{ padding: 16 }}>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#22C55E', fontWeight: 600 }}>✅ You are a member of this project.</p>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Team */}
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
              <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 14, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                <Users size={16} style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }} /> Team ({members.length})
              </h3>
              {members.map((user) => (
                user && (
                  <div key={user.id} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: '8px 0', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)' }}>
                    <div style={{ width: 32, height: 32, borderRadius: '50%', background: user.id === project.leadId ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-primary)', color: '#fff', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700 }}>
                      {getInitials(user.name)}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{user.name}</p>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                        {user.id === project.leadId ? '⭐ Project Lead' : user.department}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Mentor */}
            {mentor && (
              <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
                <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 14 }}>Faculty Mentor</h3>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
                  <div style={{ width: 38, height: 38, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'var(--color-success)', color: '#fff', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700 }}>
                    {getInitials(mentor.name)}
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{mentor.name}</p>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>{mentor.department}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Info */}
            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
              <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 14 }}>Details</h3>
              {[
                { label: 'Created', value: formatDate(project.createdAt) },
                { label: 'Last Updated', value: formatDate(project.updatedAt) },
                { label: 'Applications', value: `${project.applicationCount} total` },
                { label: 'Incubation', value: project.incubationReferred ? 'Referred to Crieya' : 'Not referred' },
              ].map(({ label, value }) => (
                <div key={label} style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', padding: '7px 0', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)', fontSize: 13 }}>
                  <span style={{ color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>{label}</span>
                  <span style={{ color: 'var(--color-text)', fontWeight: 500 }}>{value}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Join Modal */}
      <Modal open={joinOpen} onClose={() => setJoinOpen(false)} title={`Join "${project.title}"`} size="md">
        <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 20 }}>
          Tell the project lead why you want to join and what skills you bring.
        </p>
        <FormField label="Why do you want to join this project?" required>
          <textarea
            value={joinForm.message}
            onChange={(e) => setJoinForm((f) => ({ ...f, message: e.target.value }))}
            rows={4}
            className="input"
            placeholder="Describe your motivation and relevant experience..."
            style={{ resize: 'vertical', minHeight: 100 }}
          />
        </FormField>
        <FormField label="Your relevant skills (comma-separated)">
          <input
            value={joinForm.skills}
            onChange={(e) => setJoinForm((f) => ({ ...f, skills: e.target.value }))}
            className="input"
            placeholder="e.g. React, Python, UI Design"
          />
        </FormField>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 20, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
          <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => setJoinOpen(false)}>Cancel</button>
          <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={handleJoin} disabled={submitting}>
            {submitting ? 'Submitting…' : 'Submit Application'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </PublicLayout>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/public/ProjectsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { PublicLayout } from '../../layouts/PublicLayout';
import { ProjectCard } from '../../components/ui/ProjectCard';
import { SearchInput } from '../../components/ui/SearchInput';
import { FilterBar } from '../../components/ui/FilterBar';
import { EmptyState } from '../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { mockProjects } from '../../data/projects';
import { FolderKanban } from 'lucide-react';
import type { ProjectStatus, ProjectCategory } from '../../types';

const statusOptions = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'All' },
  { value: 'in_progress', label: 'Active' },
  { value: 'idea', label: 'Idea Stage' },
  { value: 'review', label: 'Under Review' },
  { value: 'completed', label: 'Completed' },
  { value: 'incubated', label: 'Incubated' },
];

const openOptions = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'All Projects' },
  { value: 'open', label: 'Open for Applications' },
];

export function ProjectsPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('all');
  const [openFilter, setOpenFilter] = useState('all');

  const filtered = mockProjects.filter((p) => {
    const matchSearch =
      !search ||
      p.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.description.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.tags.some((t) => t.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));

    const matchStatus = statusFilter === 'all' || p.status === statusFilter;
    const matchOpen = openFilter === 'all' || (openFilter === 'open' && p.isOpenForApplications);

    return matchSearch && matchStatus && matchOpen;
  });

  return (
    <PublicLayout>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 1200, margin: '0 auto', padding: '40px 24px' }}>
        <PageHeader
          title="Projects"
          subtitle="Discover what our members are building. Find your next team or project to contribute to."
        />

        {/* Filters */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, marginBottom: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
          <SearchInput
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search projects, tags…"
            style={{ width: 280 }}
          />
          <FilterBar options={statusOptions} selected={statusFilter} onChange={setStatusFilter} />
          <FilterBar options={openOptions} selected={openFilter} onChange={setOpenFilter} />
        </div>

        {/* Results */}
        {filtered.length > 0 ? (
          <>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 16 }}>
              Showing {filtered.length} project{filtered.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
            </p>
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'grid',
                gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr))',
                gap: 16,
              }}
            >
              {filtered.map((project) => (
                <ProjectCard key={project.id} project={project} href={`/projects/${project.id}`} />
              ))}
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <EmptyState
            icon={<FolderKanban size={24} />}
            title="No projects found"
            description="Try adjusting your search or filters to find what you're looking for."
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </PublicLayout>
  );
}

    statusColor: '#F59E0B',
    actor: 'Crieya',
    note: 'The outcome shown here is illustrative. Actual decisions depend on Crieya\'s independent evaluation.',
  },
];

export function WorkflowPage() {
  return (
    <PublicLayout>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 860, margin: '0 auto', padding: '40px 24px' }}>
        {/* Header */}
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 48 }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, padding: '4px 14px', background: 'var(--color-accent-light)', borderRadius: 99, marginBottom: 16 }}>
            <Lightbulb size={13} style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }} />
            <span style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-accent)' }}>Proposed Process</span>
          </div>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: 32, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 12 }}>Idea to Innovation — The Complete Workflow</h1>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 16, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.65, maxWidth: 600, margin: '0 auto' }}>
            From the spark of an idea to potential incubation, here is how the IT Club & Innovation Hub proposes to support student projects through every stage.
          </p>
          <div style={{ marginTop: 16, padding: '10px 20px', background: '#FFFBEB', border: '1px solid #FDE68A', borderRadius: 10, display: 'inline-block', maxWidth: 600 }}>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#92400E', margin: 0 }}>
              ⚠️ <strong>Note:</strong> This workflow is a proposed framework for the platform demo. Formal adoption is subject to institutional approval.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Timeline */}
        <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
          {/* Vertical line */}
          <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 28, top: 0, bottom: 0, width: 2, background: 'var(--color-border)', zIndex: 0 }} />

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 0 }}>
            {steps.map((step, i) => (
              <div key={step.id} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 20, paddingBottom: 32, position: 'relative', zIndex: 1 }}>
                {/* Icon node */}
                <div style={{ flexShrink: 0 }}>
                  <div style={{
                    width: 56, height: 56, borderRadius: '50%',
                    background: 'var(--color-surface)',
                    border: `3px solid ${step.color}`,
                    display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                    color: step.color,
                    boxShadow: '0 0 0 4px var(--color-bg)',
                  }}>
                    {step.icon}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Content */}
                <div style={{ flex: 1, paddingBottom: 4 }}>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, marginBottom: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>STEP {step.id}</span>
                    <h2 style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', flex: 1 }}>{step.title}</h2>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: 12, padding: '3px 10px', borderRadius: 99, background: `${step.statusColor}18`, color: step.statusColor, fontWeight: 600 }}>
                      {step.status}
                    </span>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', border: '1px solid var(--color-border)', padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 6 }}>
                      {step.actor}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.65, marginBottom: 12 }}>
                    {step.description}
                  </p>

                  {/* Sample */}
                  <div style={{ background: step.bg, border: `1px solid ${step.color}33`, borderRadius: 10, padding: '10px 14px', marginBottom: step.note ? 10 : 0 }}>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: step.color, marginBottom: 4, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>Sample</p>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text)', lineHeight: 1.55 }}>{step.sampleProject}</p>
                  </div>

                  {step.note && (
                    <div style={{ background: '#FFFBEB', border: '1px solid #FDE68A', borderRadius: 8, padding: '8px 12px', marginTop: 8 }}>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#92400E', margin: 0 }}>📋 {step.note}</p>

          <h2 style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 700, color: '#fff', marginBottom: 12 }}>Ready to Start Your Journey?</h2>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 15, color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)', marginBottom: 24 }}>
            Submit your idea today and take the first step toward building something impactful.
          </p>

            <Link to="/ideas/submit">
              <button className="btn" style={{ background: '#fff', color: 'var(--color-primary)', border: 'none', padding: '10px 24px', gap: 8, fontWeight: 600 }}>
                <Lightbulb size={16} /> Submit an Idea
              </button>

            <Link to="/incubation">

        </div>

FILE: src/pages/student/FindTeammatesPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { mockUsers, getUserById } from '../../data/users';
import { mockProjects } from '../../data/projects';
import { Modal } from '../../components/ui/Modal';
import { FormField } from '../../components/ui/FormField';
import { useAppStore } from '../../hooks/useAppStore';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { useToast } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { getInitials } from '../../utils';
import { Search, Filter, UserPlus, Code2, Star } from 'lucide-react';
import type { ProjectApplication } from '../../types';

const allStudents = mockUsers.filter((u) => u.role === 'student');

export function FindTeammatesPage() {
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { submitApplication } = useAppStore();
  const { addToast } = useToast();

  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [filterSkill, setFilterSkill] = useState('');
  const [contactTarget, setContactTarget] = useState<typeof allStudents[0] | null>(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const skills = Array.from(new Set(allStudents.flatMap((u) => u.skills ?? []))).sort();

  const filtered = allStudents.filter((u) => {
    if (u.id === currentUser.id) return false;
    const matchSearch = !search || u.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      u.department.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      (u.skills ?? []).some((s) => s.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));
    const matchSkill = !filterSkill || (u.skills ?? []).includes(filterSkill);
    return matchSearch && matchSkill;
  });

  // Find open projects for connecting
  const openProjects = mockProjects.filter((p) => p.isOpenForApplications);

  function handleConnect() {
    if (!contactTarget || !message.trim()) {
      addToast('error', 'Message required', 'Please write a message before sending.');
      return;
    }
    setSubmitting(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      addToast('success', 'Connection Request Sent!', `Your message has been sent to ${contactTarget.name}.`);
      setContactTarget(null);
      setMessage('');
      setSubmitting(false);
    }, 700);
  }

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Find Teammates">
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 900, marginBottom: 28 }}>
        <h1 style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 6 }}>Find Teammates</h1>
        <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
          Connect with students who have the skills your project needs.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Open Projects */}
      <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20, marginBottom: 24 }}>
        <p style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 12 }}>
          🔓 Projects Looking for Members
        </p>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          {openProjects.map((p) => (
            <Link key={p.id} to={`/projects/${p.id}`} style={{ textDecoration: 'none' }}>
              <div style={{ padding: '8px 14px', background: 'var(--color-accent-light)', border: '1px solid var(--color-accent)', borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-accent)', fontWeight: 500 }}>
                {p.title} <span style={{ opacity: 0.7 }}>({p.applicationCount} applicants)</span>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, marginBottom: 20, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 220, position: 'relative' }}>
          <Search size={15} style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 12, top: '50%', transform: 'translateY(-50%)', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }} />
          <input
            className="input"
            style={{ paddingLeft: 36 }}
            placeholder="Search by name, department, or skill…"
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
        <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
          <Filter size={14} style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 10, top: '50%', transform: 'translateY(-50%)', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }} />
          <select
            className="input"
            style={{ paddingLeft: 30, width: 180 }}
            value={filterSkill}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterSkill(e.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="">All Skills</option>
            {skills.map((s) => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Student Cards */}
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(260px, 1fr))', gap: 16 }}>
        {filtered.map((student) => (
          <div key={student.id} className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, marginBottom: 12 }}>
              <div style={{ width: 46, height: 46, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'var(--color-primary)', color: '#fff', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                {getInitials(student.name)}
              </div>
              <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>{student.name}</p>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>{student.department} {student.year ? `· Year ${student.year}` : ''}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            {student.bio && (
              <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.55, marginBottom: 12 }}>{student.bio}</p>
            )}
            {(student.skills ?? []).length > 0 && (
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap', marginBottom: 14 }}>
                {(student.skills ?? []).slice(0, 5).map((skill) => (
                  <span key={skill} style={{ padding: '3px 8px', background: 'var(--color-bg)', border: '1px solid var(--color-border)', borderRadius: 6, fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', fontWeight: 500 }}>
                    {skill}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
            {/* Open projects they're in */}
            {mockProjects.filter((p) => p.memberIds.includes(student.id)).length > 0 && (
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--color-accent)', marginBottom: 14 }}>
                <Code2 size={11} />
                {mockProjects.filter((p) => p.memberIds.includes(student.id)).length} active project(s)
              </div>
            )}
            <button
              className="btn btn-sm btn-primary"
              style={{ width: '100%', gap: 6 }}
              onClick={() => { setContactTarget(student); setMessage(''); }}
            >
              <UserPlus size={13} /> Connect
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
        {filtered.length === 0 && (
          <div style={{ gridColumn: '1/-1', textAlign: 'center', padding: 40, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
            No students match your search. Try adjusting your filters.
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Connect Modal */}
      <Modal open={!!contactTarget} onClose={() => setContactTarget(null)} title={`Connect with ${contactTarget?.name}`} size="sm">
        <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 16 }}>
          Send a message explaining why you'd like to connect or collaborate with {contactTarget?.name}.
        </p>
        <FormField label="Your Message" required>
          <textarea
            className="input"
            rows={4}
            placeholder="Hi! I'm working on a project that needs your skills in..."
            value={message}
            onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
            style={{ resize: 'vertical' }}
          />
        </FormField>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 16, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
          <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => setContactTarget(null)}>Cancel</button>
          <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={handleConnect} disabled={submitting || !message.trim()}>
            {submitting ? 'Sending…' : 'Send Message'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/student/QuizPlayerPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { PublicLayout } from '../../layouts/PublicLayout';
import { mockQuizzes } from '../../data/mock';
import { useAppStore } from '../../hooks/useAppStore';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { useToast } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { NotFoundPage } from '../NotFoundPage';
import { CheckCircle2, XCircle, ArrowRight, ArrowLeft, RotateCcw, Trophy, Clock } from 'lucide-react';

type QuizState = 'intro' | 'playing' | 'result';

export function QuizPlayerPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const quiz = mockQuizzes.find((q) => q.id === id);
  const { saveQuizResult, quizResults } = useAppStore();
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { addToast } = useToast();

  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<QuizState>('intro');
  const [currentQ, setCurrentQ] = useState(0);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Record<string, string>>({}); // questionId -> optionId
  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(0);
  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);

  const prevResult = quizResults.find((r) => r.quizId === id);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase !== 'playing') return;
    if (!quiz) return;
    setTimeLeft((quiz.estimatedMinutes ?? 5) * 60);
    timerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      setTimeLeft((prev) => {
        if (prev <= 1) {
          clearInterval(timerRef.current!);
          finishQuiz();
          return 0;
        }
        return prev - 1;
      });
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timerRef.current!);
  }, [phase]);

  if (!quiz) return <NotFoundPage />;

  // quiz is defined from here on
  const q2 = quiz;

  function finishQuiz() {
    if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current);
    const score = quiz!.questions.filter((q) => selected[q.id] === q.correctOptionId).length;
    saveQuizResult({ quizId: quiz!.id, score, total: quiz!.questions.length, completedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    setPhase('result');
    const pct = Math.round((score / quiz!.questions.length) * 100);
    addToast(pct >= 70 ? 'success' : 'info', `Quiz Complete — ${pct}%`, `You scored ${score}/${quiz!.questions.length}`);
  }

  function handleSelect(qId: string, optionId: string) {
    if (revealed.has(qId)) return;
    setSelected((prev) => ({ ...prev, [qId]: optionId }));
    setRevealed((prev) => new Set([...prev, qId]));
  }

  function handleNext() {
    if (currentQ < quiz!.questions.length - 1) {
      setCurrentQ((q) => q + 1);
    } else {
      finishQuiz();
    }
  }

  const q = quiz.questions[currentQ];
  const score = quiz.questions.filter((q) => selected[q.id] === q.correctOptionId).length;
  const pct = Math.round((score / quiz.questions.length) * 100);
  const diffColors = { beginner: { color: '#22C55E', bg: '#F0FDF4' }, intermediate: { color: '#F59E0B', bg: '#FFFBEB' }, advanced: { color: '#EF4444', bg: '#FEF2F2' } };
  const diff = diffColors[quiz.difficulty];

  const minutes = Math.floor(timeLeft / 60);
  const seconds = timeLeft % 60;

  return (
    <PublicLayout>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 720, margin: '0 auto', padding: '32px 24px' }}>
        {/* Intro */}
        {phase === 'intro' && (
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: 'center' }}>
            <div style={{ width: 64, height: 64, borderRadius: 18, background: diff.bg, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', margin: '0 auto 20px', color: diff.color }}>
              <Trophy size={30} />
            </div>
            <span className="badge" style={{ color: diff.color, background: diff.bg, marginBottom: 12, display: 'inline-block' }}>{quiz.difficulty}</span>
            <h1 style={{ fontSize: 26, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 10 }}>{quiz.title}</h1>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 15, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.6, marginBottom: 24 }}>{quiz.description}</p>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 24, marginBottom: 28 }}>
              <div style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{quiz.questions.length}</p>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>Questions</p>
              </div>
              <div style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{quiz.estimatedMinutes}</p>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>Minutes</p>
              </div>
              <div style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{quiz.category}</p>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>Topic</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            {prevResult && (
              <div style={{ background: 'var(--color-accent-light)', border: '1px solid var(--color-accent)', borderRadius: 10, padding: '10px 16px', marginBottom: 20, fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-accent)' }}>
                Your last score: <strong>{prevResult.score}/{prevResult.total}</strong> ({Math.round((prevResult.score / prevResult.total) * 100)}%)
              </div>
            )}
            <button className="btn btn-primary" style={{ fontSize: 15, padding: '12px 32px', gap: 8 }} onClick={() => { setPhase('playing'); setCurrentQ(0); setSelected({}); setRevealed(new Set()); }}>
              Start Quiz <ArrowRight size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Playing */}
        {phase === 'playing' && (
          <div>
            {/* Progress & Timer */}
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <div style={{ flex: 1, marginRight: 20 }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 6 }}>
                  <span>Question {currentQ + 1} of {quiz.questions.length}</span>
                  <span>{Math.round(((currentQ + (revealed.has(q.id) ? 1 : 0)) / quiz.questions.length) * 100)}%</span>
                </div>
                <div style={{ height: 6, background: 'var(--color-border)', borderRadius: 99 }}>
                  <div style={{ height: '100%', width: `${((currentQ) / quiz.questions.length) * 100}%`, background: 'var(--color-accent)', borderRadius: 99, transition: 'width 0.3s ease' }} />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: timeLeft < 60 ? 'var(--color-error)' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                <Clock size={15} /> {minutes}:{seconds.toString().padStart(2, '0')}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="card" style={{ padding: 28 }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 24, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
                {q.question}
              </h2>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10, marginBottom: 24 }}>
                {q.options.map((opt) => {
                  const isSelected = selected[q.id] === opt.id;
                  const isCorrect = opt.id === q.correctOptionId;
                  const isRevealed = revealed.has(q.id);
                  let bg = 'var(--color-bg)';
                  let border = 'var(--color-border)';
                  let color = 'var(--color-text)';

                  if (isRevealed) {
                    if (isCorrect) { bg = '#F0FDF4'; border = '#22C55E'; color = '#15803D'; }
                    else if (isSelected && !isCorrect) { bg = '#FEF2F2'; border = '#EF4444'; color = '#DC2626'; }
                  } else if (isSelected) {
                    bg = 'var(--color-accent-light)'; border = 'var(--color-accent)';
                  }

                  return (
                    <button
                      key={opt.id}
                      onClick={() => handleSelect(q.id, opt.id)}
                      disabled={isRevealed}
                      style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: 12,
                        padding: '12px 16px',
                        background: bg,
                        border: `1.5px solid ${border}`,
                        borderRadius: 10,
                        cursor: isRevealed ? 'default' : 'pointer',
                        textAlign: 'left',
                        fontSize: 14,
                        color,
                        fontFamily: 'inherit',
                        fontWeight: isRevealed && isCorrect ? 600 : 400,
                        transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                      }}
                    >
                      {isRevealed && isCorrect && <CheckCircle2 size={16} style={{ color: '#22C55E', flexShrink: 0 }} />}
                      {isRevealed && isSelected && !isCorrect && <XCircle size={16} style={{ color: '#EF4444', flexShrink: 0 }} />}
                      {(!isRevealed || (!isCorrect && !isSelected)) && (
                        <span style={{ width: 22, height: 22, borderRadius: '50%', border: `1.5px solid ${border}`, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                          {String.fromCharCode(65 + q.options.indexOf(opt))}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {opt.text}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {/* Explanation */}
              {revealed.has(q.id) && q.explanation && (
                <div style={{ background: 'var(--color-accent-light)', border: '1px solid var(--color-accent)', borderRadius: 10, padding: '12px 16px', marginBottom: 20, fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-accent)' }}>
                  <strong>💡 Explanation:</strong> {q.explanation}
                </div>
              )}

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onClick={() => setCurrentQ((q) => Math.max(0, q - 1))} disabled={currentQ === 0} style={{ gap: 6 }}>
                  <ArrowLeft size={14} /> Previous
                </button>
                <button
                  className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
                  onClick={handleNext}
                  disabled={!revealed.has(q.id)}
                  style={{ gap: 6 }}
                >
                  {currentQ === quiz.questions.length - 1 ? 'Finish' : 'Next'} <ArrowRight size={14} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Results */}
        {phase === 'result' && (
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: 'center' }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 60, marginBottom: 16 }}>
              {pct >= 80 ? '🏆' : pct >= 60 ? '🎯' : '📚'}
            </div>
            <h1 style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 8 }}>
              {pct >= 80 ? 'Excellent!' : pct >= 60 ? 'Good Job!' : 'Keep Practising!'}
            </h1>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 16, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 24 }}>
              You scored <strong>{score}/{quiz.questions.length}</strong> ({pct}%) on {quiz.title}
            </p>

            {/* Score Ring */}
            <div style={{ width: 120, height: 120, margin: '0 auto 28px', position: 'relative' }}>
              <svg width="120" height="120" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                <circle cx="60" cy="60" r="50" fill="none" stroke="var(--color-border)" strokeWidth="10" />
                <circle
                  cx="60" cy="60" r="50"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke={pct >= 80 ? '#22C55E' : pct >= 60 ? '#F59E0B' : '#EF4444'}
                  strokeWidth="10"
                  strokeDasharray={`${(pct / 100) * 314} 314`}
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  transform="rotate(-90 60 60)"
                  style={{ transition: 'stroke-dasharray 0.8s ease' }}
                />
              </svg>
              <div style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 26, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{pct}%</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Answer Review */}
            <div style={{ textAlign: 'left', marginBottom: 24 }}>
              <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 12 }}>Answer Review</h3>
              {quiz.questions.map((q, i) => {
                const correct = selected[q.id] === q.correctOptionId;
                return (
                  <div key={q.id} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, padding: '8px 0', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
                    {correct ? <CheckCircle2 size={16} style={{ color: '#22C55E', marginTop: 2, flexShrink: 0 }} /> : <XCircle size={16} style={{ color: '#EF4444', marginTop: 2, flexShrink: 0 }} />}
                    <div>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text)', fontWeight: 500 }}>Q{i + 1}. {q.question}</p>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: correct ? '#15803D' : '#DC2626' }}>
                        {correct ? '✓ Correct' : `✗ You chose: ${q.options.find(o => o.id === selected[q.id])?.text ?? 'No answer'}`}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, justifyContent: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
              <button
                className="btn btn-ghost"
                onClick={() => { setPhase('intro'); setSelected({}); setRevealed(new Set()); setCurrentQ(0); }}
                style={{ gap: 6 }}
              >
                <RotateCcw size={14} /> Try Again
              </button>
              <Link to="/learn">
                <button className="btn btn-primary" style={{ gap: 6 }}>Browse More Quizzes <ArrowRight size={14} /></button>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </PublicLayout>
  );
}
