FILE: src/pages/student/StudentApplicationsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/StatusBadge';
import { EmptyState } from '../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { useAppStore } from '../../hooks/useAppStore';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { getProjectById } from '../../data/projects';
import { applicationStatusConfig, formatDate } from '../../utils';
import { ClipboardList, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';

export function StudentApplicationsPage() {
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { applications } = useAppStore();

  const myApplications = applications.filter((a) => a.applicantId === currentUser.id);

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="My Applications">
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 760 }}>
        <div style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 4 }}>My Applications</h1>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
            Track your project join requests and their status.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Summary Bar */}
        {myApplications.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)', gap: 12, marginBottom: 24 }}>
            {[
              { label: 'Pending', count: myApplications.filter((a) => a.status === 'pending').length, color: '#F59E0B', bg: '#FFFBEB' },
              { label: 'Accepted', count: myApplications.filter((a) => a.status === 'accepted').length, color: '#22C55E', bg: '#F0FDF4' },
              { label: 'Rejected', count: myApplications.filter((a) => a.status === 'rejected').length, color: '#EF4444', bg: '#FEF2F2' },
            ].map((s) => (
              <div key={s.label} className="card" style={{ padding: '14px 18px', borderLeft: `4px solid ${s.color}` }}>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: s.color }}>{s.count}</p>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>{s.label}</p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {myApplications.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            icon={<ClipboardList size={28} />}
            title="No applications yet"
            description="Find projects you're interested in and apply to join the team."
            action={
              <Link to="/projects">
                <button className="btn btn-primary" style={{ gap: 6 }}>Browse Projects <ArrowRight size={14} /></button>
              </Link>
            }
          />
        ) : (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
            {myApplications.map((app) => {
              const project = getProjectById(app.projectId);
              const cfg = applicationStatusConfig[app.status];
              return (
                <div key={app.id} className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 10, marginBottom: 10 }}>
                    <div>
                      <h3 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 4 }}>
                        {project?.title ?? 'Unknown Project'}
                      </h3>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                        Applied {formatDate(app.appliedAt)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <StatusBadge {...cfg} />
                  </div>

                  <div style={{ background: 'var(--color-bg)', borderRadius: 8, padding: '10px 14px', marginBottom: 10 }}>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 4 }}>Your Message</p>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text)', lineHeight: 1.55 }}>{app.message}</p>
                  </div>

                  {app.skills.length > 0 && (
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap', marginBottom: 10 }}>
                      {app.skills.map((skill) => (
                        <span key={skill} className="badge">{skill}</span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {app.status === 'accepted' && project && (
                    <Link to={`/projects/${project.id}`}>
                      <button className="btn btn-sm btn-primary" style={{ gap: 6 }}>
                        Go to Project <ArrowRight size={13} />
                      </button>
                    </Link>
                  )}

                  {app.status === 'rejected' && (
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', fontStyle: 'italic' }}>
                      Keep exploring other projects — the right team is out there!
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        <div style={{ marginTop: 24, padding: '16px 20px', background: 'var(--color-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--color-border)', borderRadius: 12, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>Looking for new opportunities?</p>
          <Link to="/projects">
            <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style={{ gap: 6 }}>Browse Open Projects <ArrowRight size={13} /></button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/student/StudentChatPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { getProjectById } from '../../data/projects';
import { mockUsers, getUserById } from '../../data/users';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { getInitials, formatDateTime } from '../../utils';
import { Send, Pin, Users, Hash } from 'lucide-react';
import type { ChatMessage } from '../../types';

const initialMessages: ChatMessage[] = [
  { id: 'c1', projectId: 'p1', senderId: 'u2', content: 'Hey team! I just pushed the RFID integration to the dev branch. Can someone review?', sentAt: '2026-10-22T08:15:00', readBy: ['u1', 'u7'] },
  { id: 'c2', projectId: 'p1', senderId: 'u7', content: 'On it! Will take a look after my morning class 🙌', sentAt: '2026-10-22T08:32:00', readBy: ['u1', 'u2'] },
  { id: 'c3', projectId: 'p1', senderId: 'u1', content: 'Reviewed and merged. Great work Priya! The RFID is reading cards consistently now.', sentAt: '2026-10-22T10:00:00', readBy: ['u2', 'u7'] },
  { id: 'c4', projectId: 'p1', senderId: 'u2', content: 'Thanks! Next up: connecting it to the backend API. ETA end of week.', sentAt: '2026-10-22T10:05:00', readBy: ['u1'] },
  { id: 'c5', projectId: 'p1', senderId: 'u7', content: 'Dashboard UI is 60% done. Should be ready for the demo on Friday.', sentAt: '2026-10-22T11:30:00', readBy: ['u1', 'u2'] },
  { id: 'c6', projectId: 'p1', senderId: 'u1', content: 'Perfect. Mentor session is tomorrow at 3 PM. Please prepare your progress slides.', sentAt: '2026-10-22T12:00:00', readBy: ['u2', 'u7'] },
];

const pinnedMessage = '📌 Next mentor session: Oct 25, 3:00 PM. Join via Google Meet. Prepare progress updates!';

export function StudentChatPage() {
  const { projectId } = useParams<{ projectId: string }>();
  const pid = projectId ?? 'p1';
  const project = getProjectById(pid);
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();

  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>(initialMessages.filter((m) => m.projectId === 'p1'));
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [showMembers, setShowMembers] = useState(false);
  const bottomRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [messages]);

  function handleSend() {
    const text = input.trim();
    if (!text) return;
    const msg: ChatMessage = {
      id: `c-${Date.now()}`,
      projectId: pid,
      senderId: currentUser.id,
      content: text,
      sentAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      readBy: [],
    };
    setMessages((prev) => [...prev, msg]);
    setInput('');
  }

  const members = project ? project.memberIds.map((id) => getUserById(id)).filter(Boolean) : [];

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Project Chat">
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 20, height: 'calc(100vh - 160px)', alignItems: 'stretch' }}>
        {/* Chat Panel */}
        <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', background: 'var(--color-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--color-border)', borderRadius: 14, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
          {/* Chat Header */}
          <div style={{ padding: '14px 20px', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ width: 36, height: 36, borderRadius: 10, background: 'var(--color-primary)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
              <Hash size={18} color="#fff" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{project?.title ?? 'Project Chat'}</p>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>{members.length} members</p>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowMembers((p) => !p)}
              className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm"
              style={{ marginLeft: 'auto', gap: 6 }}
            >
              <Users size={14} /> Members
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Pinned Message */}
          <div style={{ padding: '8px 20px', background: '#FFFBEB', borderBottom: '1px solid #FDE68A', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
            <Pin size={13} style={{ color: '#F59E0B', flexShrink: 0 }} />
            <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#92400E' }}>{pinnedMessage}</p>
          </div>

          {/* Messages */}
          <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', padding: '20px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
            {messages.map((msg, i) => {
              const sender = getUserById(msg.senderId);
              const isMe = msg.senderId === currentUser.id;
              const prevMsg = messages[i - 1];
              const showSender = !prevMsg || prevMsg.senderId !== msg.senderId;

              return (
                <div key={msg.id} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, flexDirection: isMe ? 'row-reverse' : 'row', alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                  {!isMe && showSender && (
                    <div style={{ width: 30, height: 30, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'var(--color-primary)', color: '#fff', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                      {sender ? getInitials(sender.name) : '?'}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {!isMe && !showSender && <div style={{ width: 30, flexShrink: 0 }} />}
                  <div style={{ maxWidth: '65%' }}>
                    {showSender && !isMe && (
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 4 }}>
                        {sender?.name ?? 'Unknown'}
                      </p>
                    )}
                    <div style={{
                      padding: '10px 14px',
                      borderRadius: isMe ? '14px 14px 4px 14px' : '14px 14px 14px 4px',
                      background: isMe ? 'var(--color-primary)' : 'var(--color-bg)',
                      border: isMe ? 'none' : '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                      color: isMe ? '#fff' : 'var(--color-text)',
                      fontSize: 14,
                      lineHeight: 1.55,
                    }}>
                      {msg.content}
                    </div>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginTop: 4, textAlign: isMe ? 'right' : 'left' }}>
                      {new Date(msg.sentAt).toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
            <div ref={bottomRef} />
          </div>

          {/* Input */}
          <div style={{ padding: '12px 20px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--color-border)', display: 'flex', gap: 10 }}>
            <input
              className="input"
              style={{ flex: 1 }}
              placeholder="Type a message… (press Enter to send)"
              value={input}
              onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); handleSend(); } }}
            />
            <button
              className="btn btn-primary"
              onClick={handleSend}
              disabled={!input.trim()}
              style={{ gap: 6, flexShrink: 0 }}
            >
              <Send size={15} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Members Panel */}
        {showMembers && (
          <div style={{ width: 220, background: 'var(--color-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--color-border)', borderRadius: 14, padding: 16, flexShrink: 0 }}>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 14 }}>Members</p>
            {members.map((user) => (
              user && (
                <div key={user.id} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: '8px 0', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)' }}>
                  <div style={{ width: 30, height: 30, borderRadius: '50%', background: user.id === project?.leadId ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-primary)', color: '#fff', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700 }}>
                    {getInitials(user.name)}
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>{user.name}</p>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: user.id === project?.leadId ? 'var(--color-accent)' : 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                      {user.id === project?.leadId ? 'Lead' : user.role}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: '50%', background: '#22C55E', marginLeft: 'auto', flexShrink: 0 }} />
                </div>
              )
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/student/StudentDashboard.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { StatCard } from '../../components/ui/StatCard';
import { ProjectCard } from '../../components/ui/ProjectCard';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/StatusBadge';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { useAppStore } from '../../hooks/useAppStore';
import { getProjectsByMember } from '../../data/projects';
import { getProjectById } from '../../data/projects';
import { mockEvents } from '../../data/mock';
import { applicationStatusConfig, eventTypeConfig, formatDate, getDaysUntil, taskStatusConfig } from '../../utils';
import {
  FolderKanban, ClipboardList, Calendar, CheckSquare,
  ArrowRight, Lightbulb, Trophy, Users, MessageSquare
} from 'lucide-react';

export function StudentDashboard() {
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { applications, tasks, registeredEventIds } = useAppStore();
  const myProjects = getProjectsByMember(currentUser.id);
  const myApplications = applications.filter((a) => a.applicantId === currentUser.id);
  const upcomingEvents = mockEvents.filter((e) => getDaysUntil(e.startDate) > 0).slice(0, 3);
  const myTasks = tasks.filter((t) => t.assigneeId === currentUser.id);

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Dashboard">
      <PageHeader
        title={`Welcome back, ${currentUser.name.split(' ')[0]}! 👋`}
        subtitle={`${currentUser.department} · ${currentUser.year ? `Year ${currentUser.year}` : ''}`}
      />

      {/* Stats */}
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(200px, 1fr))', gap: 16, marginBottom: 28 }}>
        <StatCard
          label="My Projects"
          value={myProjects.length}
          icon={<FolderKanban size={20} />}
          color="var(--color-accent)"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Applications"
          value={myApplications.length}
          icon={<ClipboardList size={20} />}
          color="var(--color-warning)"
          description={`${myApplications.filter(a => a.status === 'accepted').length} accepted`}
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Events Registered"
          value={registeredEventIds.size}
          icon={<Calendar size={20} />}
          color="var(--color-success)"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Tasks Assigned"
          value={myTasks.length}
          icon={<CheckSquare size={20} />}
          color="var(--color-incubated)"
          description={`${myTasks.filter(t => t.status === 'done').length} completed`}
        />
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 340px', gap: 20, alignItems: 'start' }}>
        {/* Left Column */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 20 }}>
          {/* My Projects */}
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700 }}>My Projects</h2>
              <Link to="/student/projects">
                <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style={{ gap: 4 }}>
                  View all <ArrowRight size={13} />
                </button>
              </Link>
            </div>
            {myProjects.length > 0 ? (
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
                {myProjects.slice(0, 2).map((p) => (
                  <ProjectCard key={p.id} project={p} compact href={`/student/projects/${p.id}`} />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p style={{ color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', fontSize: 14 }}>
                You haven't joined any projects yet.{' '}
                <Link to="/projects" style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }}>
                  Browse projects
                </Link>
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* My Tasks */}
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700 }}>My Tasks</h2>
            </div>
            {myTasks.length > 0 ? (
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 1 }}>
                {myTasks.map((task) => {
                  const cfg = taskStatusConfig[task.status];
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={task.id}
                      style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: 12,
                        padding: '10px 0',
                        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                      }}
                    >
                      <StatusBadge {...cfg} size="sm" />
                      <span style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>{task.title}</span>
                      {task.dueDate && (
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                          Due {formatDate(task.dueDate)}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p style={{ color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', fontSize: 14 }}>No tasks assigned to you.</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Column */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
          {/* Applications */}
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 14 }}>Applications</h2>
            {myApplications.length > 0 ? (
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>
                {myApplications.map((app) => {
                  const cfg = applicationStatusConfig[app.status];
                  const proj = getProjectById(app.projectId);
                  return (
                    <div key={app.id} style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text)', flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                        {proj?.title ?? app.projectId}
                      </span>
                      <StatusBadge {...cfg} size="sm" dot={false} />
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                No applications yet.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Upcoming Events */}
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 14 }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700 }}>Upcoming Events</h2>
              <Link to="/student/events">
                <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style={{ gap: 4 }}>
                  All <ArrowRight size={13} />
                </button>
              </Link>
            </div>
            {upcomingEvents.map((event) => {
              const cfg = eventTypeConfig[event.type];
              return (
                <div
                  key={event.id}
                  style={{
                    padding: '10px 0',
                    borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                    display: 'flex',
                    gap: 10,
                    alignItems: 'flex-start',
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      width: 36,
                      height: 36,
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      background: cfg.bg,
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                      flexShrink: 0,
                    }}
                  >
                    <Calendar size={16} style={{ color: cfg.color }} />
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 2, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                      {event.title}
                    </p>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                      {formatDate(event.startDate)}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {/* Quick Links */}
          <div className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 14 }}>Quick Actions</h2>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 8 }}>
              {[
                { to: '/projects', label: 'Browse & Join Projects', icon: <FolderKanban size={14} /> },
                { to: '/ideas/submit', label: 'Submit an Idea', icon: <Lightbulb size={14} /> },
                { to: '/student/teammates', label: 'Find Teammates', icon: <Users size={14} /> },
                { to: '/student/chat', label: 'Project Chat', icon: <MessageSquare size={14} /> },
                { to: '/student/learn', label: 'Take a Quiz', icon: <Trophy size={14} /> },
              ].map((link) => (
                <Link
                  key={link.to}
                  to={link.to}
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    gap: 8,
                    padding: '8px 12px',
                    borderRadius: 8,
                    fontSize: 13,
                    color: 'var(--color-text)',
                    background: 'var(--color-bg)',
                    border: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                    textDecoration: 'none',
                    transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                  }}
                >
                  <span style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }}>{link.icon}</span>
                  {link.label}
                  <ArrowRight size={12} style={{ marginLeft: 'auto', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }} />
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <style>{`
        @media (max-width: 900px) {
          .dashboard-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr !important; }
        }
      `}</style>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/student/StudentEventsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { EventCard } from '../../components/ui/EventCard';
import { mockEvents } from '../../data/mock';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { FilterBar } from '../../components/ui/FilterBar';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { getDaysUntil } from '../../utils';

const tabs = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'All Events' },
  { value: 'registered', label: 'Registered' },
  { value: 'upcoming', label: 'Upcoming' },
];

export function StudentEventsPage() {
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('all');

  const filtered = mockEvents.filter((e) => {
    if (tab === 'registered') return e.registeredIds.includes(currentUser.id);
    if (tab === 'upcoming') return getDaysUntil(e.startDate) > 0;
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Events">
      <PageHeader title="Events" subtitle="Hackathons, workshops, and more." />
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
        <FilterBar options={tabs} selected={tab} onChange={setTab} />
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr))', gap: 16 }}>
        {filtered.map((event) => (
          <EventCard
            key={event.id}
            event={event}
            registered={event.registeredIds.includes(currentUser.id)}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/student/StudentIdeasPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/StatusBadge';
import { EmptyState } from '../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { useAppStore } from '../../hooks/useAppStore';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { ideaStatusConfig, formatDate } from '../../utils';
import { Lightbulb, Plus, ThumbsUp, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';

export function StudentIdeasPage() {
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { ideas } = useAppStore();

  const myIdeas = ideas.filter((i) => i.submittedBy === currentUser.id);

  const statusSummary = {
    pending: myIdeas.filter((i) => i.status === 'pending').length,
    approved: myIdeas.filter((i) => i.status === 'approved' || i.status === 'converted').length,
    rejected: myIdeas.filter((i) => i.status === 'rejected').length,
  };

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="My Ideas">
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 760 }}>
        {/* Header */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', marginBottom: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 12 }}>
          <div>
            <h1 style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 4 }}>My Ideas</h1>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
              Track ideas you've submitted for faculty review.
            </p>
          </div>
          <Link to="/ideas/submit">
            <button className="btn btn-primary" style={{ gap: 6 }}>
              <Plus size={15} /> Submit New Idea
            </button>
          </Link>
        </div>

        {/* Summary */}
        {myIdeas.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)', gap: 12, marginBottom: 24 }}>
            {[
              { label: 'Pending Review', count: statusSummary.pending, color: '#F59E0B', bg: '#FFFBEB' },
              { label: 'Approved', count: statusSummary.approved, color: '#22C55E', bg: '#F0FDF4' },
              { label: 'Rejected', count: statusSummary.rejected, color: '#EF4444', bg: '#FEF2F2' },
            ].map((s) => (
              <div key={s.label} className="card" style={{ padding: '14px 18px', borderLeft: `4px solid ${s.color}` }}>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: s.color }}>{s.count}</p>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>{s.label}</p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Ideas List */}
        {myIdeas.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            icon={<Lightbulb size={28} />}
            title="No ideas submitted yet"
            description="Have a great idea? Share it with the club and get faculty feedback."
            action={
              <Link to="/ideas/submit">
                <button className="btn btn-primary" style={{ gap: 6 }}>
                  <Plus size={15} /> Submit Your First Idea
                </button>
              </Link>
            }
          />
        ) : (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
            {myIdeas.map((idea) => {
              const cfg = ideaStatusConfig[idea.status];
              return (
                <div key={idea.id} className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginBottom: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    <StatusBadge {...cfg} />
                    <span className="badge">{idea.category}</span>
                    <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                      <ThumbsUp size={11} /> {idea.votes} votes
                    </span>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginLeft: 'auto' }}>
                      {formatDate(idea.submittedAt)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 6 }}>{idea.title}</h3>
                  <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
                    {idea.description.slice(0, 160)}{idea.description.length > 160 ? '…' : ''}
                  </p>
                  {idea.reviewNote && (
                    <div style={{
                      marginTop: 12,
                      padding: '10px 14px',
                      background: idea.status === 'rejected' ? '#FEF2F2' : 'var(--color-accent-light)',
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      borderLeft: `3px solid ${idea.status === 'rejected' ? '#EF4444' : 'var(--color-accent)'}`,
                    }}>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: idea.status === 'rejected' ? '#DC2626' : 'var(--color-accent)', marginBottom: 4 }}>
                        Faculty Feedback
                      </p>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text)', lineHeight: 1.5 }}>{idea.reviewNote}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {idea.status === 'approved' && (
                    <div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
                      <Link to="/student/teammates">
                        <button className="btn btn-sm btn-primary" style={{ gap: 6 }}>
                          Find Teammates <ArrowRight size={13} />
                        </button>
                      </Link>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Browse All Ideas */}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 24, padding: '16px 20px', background: 'var(--color-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--color-border)', borderRadius: 12, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>See what other students are building</p>
          <Link to="/ideas">
            <button className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style={{ gap: 6 }}>Browse All Ideas <ArrowRight size={13} /></button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/student/StudentLearnPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { ArticleCard } from '../../components/ui/ArticleCard';
import { mockBlogPosts, mockQuizzes } from '../../data/mock';
import { FilterBar } from '../../components/ui/FilterBar';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Clock, Zap } from 'lucide-react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppStore } from '../../hooks/useAppStore';

const tabs = [
  { value: 'articles', label: 'Articles' },
  { value: 'quizzes', label: 'Quizzes' },
];

const difficultyColors = {
  beginner: { color: '#22C55E', bg: '#F0FDF4' },
  intermediate: { color: '#F59E0B', bg: '#FFFBEB' },
  advanced: { color: '#EF4444', bg: '#FEF2F2' },
};

export function StudentLearnPage() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('articles');
  const { quizResults } = useAppStore();

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Learn">
      <PageHeader title="Learn" subtitle="Grow your skills with curated articles and quizzes." />
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
        <FilterBar options={tabs} selected={tab} onChange={setTab} />
      </div>
      {tab === 'articles' && (
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr))', gap: 16 }}>
          {mockBlogPosts.map((post) => (
            <ArticleCard key={post.id} post={post} href={`/learn/${post.slug}`} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {tab === 'quizzes' && (
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr))', gap: 16 }}>
          {mockQuizzes.map((quiz) => {
            const diff = difficultyColors[quiz.difficulty];
            const prevResult = quizResults.find((r) => r.quizId === quiz.id);
            return (
              <div key={quiz.id} className="card card-hover" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 }}>
                  <span className="badge" style={{ color: diff.color, background: diff.bg }}>{quiz.difficulty}</span>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', alignSelf: 'center' }}>{quiz.category}</span>
                </div>
                <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 8 }}>{quiz.title}</h3>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 12, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>{quiz.description}</p>
                {prevResult && (
                  <div style={{ padding: '6px 10px', background: 'var(--color-accent-light)', borderRadius: 6, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-accent)', marginBottom: 12, fontWeight: 500 }}>
                    Last score: {prevResult.score}/{prevResult.total} ({Math.round((prevResult.score / prevResult.total) * 100)}%) ✓
                  </div>
                )}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                    <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}><Zap size={12} /> {quiz.questions.length} questions</span>
                    <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}><Clock size={12} /> {quiz.estimatedMinutes} min</span>
                  </div>
                  <Link to={`/student/quiz/${quiz.id}`}>
                    <button className="btn btn-primary btn-sm">{prevResult ? 'Retry' : 'Start Quiz'}</button>
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';

export function StudentProjectsPage() {
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const { addToast } = useToast();
  const myProjects = getProjectsByMember(currentUser.id);

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="My Projects">
      <PageHeader
        title="My Projects"
        subtitle="Projects you lead or are a member of."
        action={
          <button
            className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
            onClick={() => addToast('info', 'Coming Soon', 'Project creation will be available in Phase 2.')}
          >
            <Plus size={15} /> New Project
          </button>
        }
      />
      {myProjects.length > 0 ? (
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr))', gap: 16 }}>
          {myProjects.map((p) => (
            <ProjectCard key={p.id} project={p} href={`/student/projects/${p.id}`} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<FolderKanban size={24} />}
          title="No projects yet"
          description="Join an existing project or create a new one to get started."

        />
      )}

}

FILE: src/pages/student/StudentSessionsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { DashboardLayout } from '../../layouts/DashboardLayout';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/PageHeader';
import { mockSessions } from '../../data/mock';
import { useDemoRole } from '../../hooks/useAppContext';
import { formatDateTime } from '../../utils';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/StatusBadge';
import { getUserById } from '../../data/users';
import { getInitials } from '../../utils';
import { Video } from 'lucide-react';

export function StudentSessionsPage() {
  const { currentUser } = useDemoRole();
  const mySessions = mockSessions.filter((s) => s.studentIds.includes(currentUser.id));

  return (
    <DashboardLayout title="Sessions">
      <PageHeader title="Mentorship Sessions" subtitle="Your scheduled and completed sessions with faculty mentors." />
      {mySessions.length > 0 ? (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
          {mySessions.map((session) => {
            const mentor = getUserById(session.mentorId);
            const statusCfg = {
              scheduled: { label: 'Scheduled', color: 'var(--color-accent)', bg: 'var(--color-accent-light)' },
              completed: { label: 'Completed', color: 'var(--color-success)', bg: '#F0FDF4' },
              cancelled: { label: 'Cancelled', color: 'var(--color-error)', bg: '#FEF2F2' },
            }[session.status];

            return (
              <div key={session.id} className="card" style={{ padding: 20 }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: 12, marginBottom: 12 }}>
                  <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 600 }}>{session.title}</h3>
                  <StatusBadge {...statusCfg} size="sm" />
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                  <span>📅 {formatDateTime(session.scheduledAt)}</span>
                  <span>⏱ {session.durationMinutes} min</span>
                  <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
                    <div style={{ width: 20, height: 20, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'var(--color-primary)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: 9, fontWeight: 700, color: '#fff' }}>
                      {mentor ? getInitials(mentor.name) : '?'}
                    </div>
                    {mentor?.name ?? 'Unknown Mentor'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                {session.notes && (
                  <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', background: 'var(--color-bg)', padding: '8px 12px', borderRadius: 8 }}>
                    <strong>Notes:</strong> {session.notes}
                  </p>
                )}
                {session.meetLink && session.status === 'scheduled' && (
                  <a href={session.meetLink} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{ display: 'inline-block', marginTop: 10 }}>
                    <button className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"><Video size={13} /> Join Meeting</button>
                  </a>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '60px 0', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 500, marginBottom: 8 }}>No sessions yet</p>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 14 }}>Sessions will appear here once scheduled by your mentor.</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}

                <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 16 }}>{r.subtitle}</p>
                <ul style={{ listStyle: 'none', padding: 0, margin: '0 0 20px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6 }}>
                  {r.perks.map((perk) => (
                    <li key={perk} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--color-text)' }}>
                      <span style={{ width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: '50%', background: r.accentColor, flexShrink: 0 }} />
                      {perk}
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
                <Link to={r.path} onClick={() => switchRole(r.role)}>
                  <button
                    style={{
                      width: '100%',
                      padding: '10px 20px',
                      background: isActive ? r.accentColor : 'transparent',
                      color: isActive ? '#fff' : r.accentColor,
                      border: `1.5px solid ${r.accentColor}`,
                      borderRadius: 10,
                      fontSize: 14,
                      fontWeight: 600,
                      cursor: 'pointer',
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                      gap: 8,
                      transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                      fontFamily: 'inherit',
                    }}
                  >
                    {isActive ? 'Go to Dashboard' : `Enter as ${r.label}`}
                    <ArrowRight size={15} />
                  </button>
                </Link>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Quick Links */}
        <div style={{ background: 'var(--color-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--color-border)', borderRadius: 16, padding: 24, marginBottom: 48 }}>
          <h3 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--color-text)', marginBottom: 16, textAlign: 'left' }}>Quick Preview Links</h3>
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr))', gap: 10 }}>
            {[
              { to: '/projects', label: 'Browse Projects', icon: <Code2 size={14} /> },
              { to: '/ideas/submit', label: 'Submit an Idea', icon: <Lightbulb size={14} /> },
              { to: '/workflow', label: 'Idea-to-Project Workflow', icon: <Zap size={14} /> },
              { to: '/learn', label: 'Knowledge Hub', icon: <BookOpen size={14} /> },
              { to: '/incubation', label: 'Crieya Incubation Corner', icon: <ArrowRight size={14} /> },
            ].map((link) => (
              <Link
                key={link.to}
                to={link.to}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  gap: 8,
                  padding: '10px 14px',
                  background: 'var(--color-bg)',
                  border: '1px solid var(--color-border)',
                  borderRadius: 10,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  color: 'var(--color-text)',
                  textDecoration: 'none',
                  fontWeight: 500,
                  transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                }}
              >
                <span style={{ color: 'var(--color-accent)' }}>{link.icon}</span>
                {link.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ─── User & Auth ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type Role = 'student' | 'faculty' | 'admin';

export interface User {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: Role;
  avatar?: string;
  department: string;
  year?: number; // for students
  bio?: string;
  skills?: string[];
  joinedAt: string;
}

// ─── Project ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type ProjectStatus =
  | 'idea'
  | 'in_progress'
  | 'review'
  | 'completed'
  | 'incubated';

export type ProjectCategory =
  | 'Web Development'
  | 'Mobile App'
  | 'AI / ML'
  | 'IoT'
  | 'Cybersecurity'
  | 'Data Science'
  | 'Blockchain'
  | 'Game Dev'
  | 'Open Source'
  | 'Other';

export interface Project {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  longDescription?: string;
  category: ProjectCategory;
  status: ProjectStatus;
  tags: string[];
  leadId: string;
  mentorId?: string;
  memberIds: string[];
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  thumbnail?: string;
  githubUrl?: string;
  demoUrl?: string;
  isOpenForApplications: boolean;
  applicationCount: number;
  incubationReferred: boolean;
}

// ─── Idea ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type IdeaStatus = 'pending' | 'approved' | 'rejected' | 'converted';

export interface Idea {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  category: ProjectCategory;
  submittedBy: string;
  submittedAt: string;
  status: IdeaStatus;
  votes: number;
  votedBy: string[];
  reviewNote?: string;
  convertedProjectId?: string;
}

// ─── Project Application ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type ApplicationStatus = 'pending' | 'accepted' | 'rejected';

export interface ProjectApplication {
  id: string;
  projectId: string;
  applicantId: string;
  message: string;
  skills: string[];
  appliedAt: string;
  status: ApplicationStatus;
}

// ─── Task ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type TaskStatus = 'todo' | 'in_progress' | 'done';
export type TaskPriority = 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';

export interface Task {
  id: string;
  projectId: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  assigneeId?: string;
  status: TaskStatus;
  priority: TaskPriority;
  dueDate?: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

// ─── Blog / Article ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface BlogPost {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  slug: string;
  excerpt: string;
  content: string;
  authorId: string;
  tags: string[];
  category: string;
  publishedAt: string;
  readingTime: number; // minutes
  coverImage?: string;
  featured: boolean;
  views: number;
}

// ─── Quiz ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface QuizOption {
  id: string;
  text: string;
}

export interface QuizQuestion {
  id: string;
  question: string;
  options: QuizOption[];
  correctOptionId: string;
  explanation?: string;
}

export interface Quiz {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  category: string;
  difficulty: 'beginner' | 'intermediate' | 'advanced';
  questions: QuizQuestion[];
  createdAt: string;
  authorId: string;
  estimatedMinutes: number;
}

// ─── Event ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type EventType = 'workshop' | 'hackathon' | 'talk' | 'meetup' | 'competition';

export interface Event {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  type: EventType;
  startDate: string;
  endDate: string;
  location: string;
  isOnline: boolean;
  organizerId: string;
  registeredIds: string[];
  maxCapacity?: number;
  tags: string[];
  coverImage?: string;
  registrationDeadline?: string;
}

// ─── Session / Mentorship ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type SessionStatus = 'scheduled' | 'completed' | 'cancelled';

export interface Session {
  id: string;
  projectId: string;
  mentorId: string;
  studentIds: string[];
  title: string;
  scheduledAt: string;
  durationMinutes: number;
  status: SessionStatus;
  meetLink?: string;
  notes?: string;
}

// ─── Notification ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type NotificationType =
  | 'project_update'
  | 'application_received'
  | 'application_status'
  | 'idea_reviewed'
  | 'session_scheduled'
  | 'event_reminder'
  | 'task_assigned'
  | 'mention';

export interface Notification {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  type: NotificationType;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  read: boolean;
  createdAt: string;
  actionUrl?: string;
}

// ─── Chat Message ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ChatMessage {

  projectId: string;
  senderId: string;
  content: string;
  sentAt: string;
  readBy: string[];
}

// ─── UI Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type ToastVariant = 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info';

export interface ToastMessage {
  id: string;
  variant: ToastVariant;
  title: string;
  message?: string;
}

import type { ProjectStatus, IdeaStatus, ApplicationStatus, TaskStatus, EventType } from '../types';

export function formatDate(dateStr: string): string {
  const date = new Date(dateStr);
  return date.toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  });
}

export function formatDateTime(dateStr: string): string {
  const date = new Date(dateStr);
  return date.toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });
}

export function timeAgo(dateStr: string): string {
  const now = new Date();
  const date = new Date(dateStr);
  const diffMs = now.getTime() - date.getTime();
  const diffDays = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  const diffHours = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60));
  const diffMins = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60));

  if (diffMins < 1) return 'just now';
  if (diffMins < 60) return `${diffMins}m ago`;
  if (diffHours < 24) return `${diffHours}h ago`;
  if (diffDays < 7) return `${diffDays}d ago`;
  return formatDate(dateStr);
}

export function getDaysUntil(dateStr: string): number {
  const now = new Date();
  const date = new Date(dateStr);
  return Math.ceil((date.getTime() - now.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
}

  hackathon: { label: 'Hackathon', color: '#8B5CF6', bg: '#F5F3FF' },
  talk: { label: 'Talk', color: '#F59E0B', bg: '#FFFBEB' },
  meetup: { label: 'Meetup', color: '#22C55E', bg: '#F0FDF4' },
  competition: { label: 'Competition', color: '#EF4444', bg: '#FEF2F2' },

export function getInitials(name: string): string {
  return name
    .split(' ')
    .map((n) => n[0])
    .join('')
    .toUpperCase()
    .slice(0, 2);
}

export function cn(...classes: (string | undefined | null | false)[]): string {
  return classes.filter(Boolean).join(' ');
}

export function truncate(str: string, length: number): string {
  if (str.length <= length) return str;
  return str.slice(0, length) + '…';
}

import React from 'react';
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DemoRoleProvider, ToastProvider } from './hooks/useAppContext';
import { AppStoreProvider } from './hooks/useAppStore';
import { ToastContainer } from './components/ui/Toast';
import { DemoRoleSwitcher } from './components/DemoRoleSwitcher';

// ── Public Pages ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import { HomePage } from './pages/public/HomePage';
import { ProjectsPage } from './pages/public/ProjectsPage';
import { ProjectDetailPage } from './pages/public/ProjectDetailPage';
import { EventsPage } from './pages/public/EventsPage';
import { EventDetailPage } from './pages/public/EventDetailPage';
import { LearnPage } from './pages/public/LearnPage';
import { ArticlePage } from './pages/public/ArticlePage';
import { IdeasPage } from './pages/public/IdeasPage';
import { IdeaSubmitPage } from './pages/public/IdeaSubmitPage';
import { WorkflowPage } from './pages/public/WorkflowPage';
import { IncubationPage } from './pages/public/IncubationPage';
import { DemoEntryPage } from './pages/DemoEntryPage';
import { NotFoundPage, ComingSoonPage } from './pages/NotFoundPage';

// ── Student Pages ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import { StudentDashboard } from './pages/student/StudentDashboard';
import { StudentProjectsPage } from './pages/student/StudentProjectsPage';
import { StudentApplicationsPage } from './pages/student/StudentApplicationsPage';
import { StudentEventsPage } from './pages/student/StudentEventsPage';
import { StudentIdeasPage } from './pages/student/StudentIdeasPage';
import { StudentSessionsPage } from './pages/student/StudentSessionsPage';
import { StudentLearnPage } from './pages/student/StudentLearnPage';
import { QuizPlayerPage } from './pages/student/QuizPlayerPage';
import { FindTeammatesPage } from './pages/student/FindTeammatesPage';
import { StudentChatPage } from './pages/student/StudentChatPage';

// ── Faculty Pages ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import { FacultyDashboard } from './pages/faculty/FacultyDashboard';
import { FacultyProjectsPage } from './pages/faculty/FacultyProjectsPage';
import { FacultySessionsPage } from './pages/faculty/FacultySessionsPage';
import { FacultyIdeasPage } from './pages/faculty/FacultyIdeasPage';
import { FacultyStudentsPage } from './pages/faculty/FacultyStudentsPage';

// ── Admin Pages ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import { AdminDashboard } from './pages/admin/AdminDashboard';
import { AdminUsersPage } from './pages/admin/AdminUsersPage';
import { AdminProjectsPage } from './pages/admin/AdminProjectsPage';
import { AdminIdeasPage } from './pages/admin/AdminIdeasPage';
import { AdminEventsPage } from './pages/admin/AdminEventsPage';
import { AdminAnalyticsPage } from './pages/admin/AdminAnalyticsPage';
import { AdminAnnouncementsPage } from './pages/admin/AdminAnnouncementsPage';

function AppInner() {
  return (
    <>
      <Routes>
        {/* ── Public Routes ── */}
        <Route path="/" element={<HomePage />} />
        <Route path="/demo" element={<DemoEntryPage />} />

        {/* Projects */}
        <Route path="/projects" element={<ProjectsPage />} />
        <Route path="/projects/:id" element={<ProjectDetailPage />} />

        {/* Events */}
        <Route path="/events" element={<EventsPage />} />
        <Route path="/events/:id" element={<EventDetailPage />} />

        {/* Learn */}
        <Route path="/learn" element={<LearnPage />} />
        <Route path="/learn/:slug" element={<ArticlePage />} />
        <Route path="/learn/quiz/:id" element={<QuizPlayerPage />} />

        {/* Ideas */}
        <Route path="/ideas" element={<IdeasPage />} />
        <Route path="/ideas/submit" element={<IdeaSubmitPage />} />

        {/* Info Pages */}
        <Route path="/workflow" element={<WorkflowPage />} />
        <Route path="/incubation" element={<IncubationPage />} />

        {/* ── Student Routes ── */}
        <Route path="/student/dashboard" element={<StudentDashboard />} />
        <Route path="/student/projects" element={<StudentProjectsPage />} />
        <Route path="/student/projects/:id" element={<ProjectDetailPage />} />
        <Route path="/student/applications" element={<StudentApplicationsPage />} />
        <Route path="/student/events" element={<StudentEventsPage />} />
        <Route path="/student/ideas" element={<StudentIdeasPage />} />
        <Route path="/student/sessions" element={<StudentSessionsPage />} />
        <Route path="/student/learn" element={<StudentLearnPage />} />
        <Route path="/student/quiz/:id" element={<QuizPlayerPage />} />
        <Route path="/student/teammates" element={<FindTeammatesPage />} />
        <Route path="/student/chat/:projectId" element={<StudentChatPage />} />
        <Route path="/student/chat" element={<StudentChatPage />} />

        {/* ── Faculty Routes ── */}
        <Route path="/faculty/dashboard" element={<FacultyDashboard />} />
        <Route path="/faculty/projects" element={<FacultyProjectsPage />} />
        <Route path="/faculty/projects/:id" element={<ProjectDetailPage />} />
        <Route path="/faculty/sessions" element={<FacultySessionsPage />} />
        <Route path="/faculty/ideas" element={<FacultyIdeasPage />} />
        <Route path="/faculty/students" element={<FacultyStudentsPage />} />

        {/* ── Admin Routes ── */}
        <Route path="/admin/dashboard" element={<AdminDashboard />} />
        <Route path="/admin/users" element={<AdminUsersPage />} />
        <Route path="/admin/projects" element={<AdminProjectsPage />} />
        <Route path="/admin/ideas" element={<AdminIdeasPage />} />
        <Route path="/admin/events" element={<AdminEventsPage />} />
        <Route path="/admin/analytics" element={<AdminAnalyticsPage />} />
        <Route path="/admin/announcements" element={<AdminAnnouncementsPage />} />
        <Route path="/admin/reports" element={<AdminAnalyticsPage />} />

        {/* ── Fallbacks ── */}
        <Route path="/student" element={<Navigate to="/student/dashboard" replace />} />
        <Route path="/faculty" element={<Navigate to="/faculty/dashboard" replace />} />
        <Route path="/admin" element={<Navigate to="/admin/dashboard" replace />} />
        <Route path="*" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
      </Routes>

      {/* Global UI */}
      <DemoRoleSwitcher />
      <ToastContainer />
    </>
  );
}

function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <ToastProvider>
        <DemoRoleProvider>
          <AppStoreProvider>
            <AppInner />
          </AppStoreProvider>
        </DemoRoleProvider>
      </ToastProvider>
    </BrowserRouter>
  );
}

export default App;

================================================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================================================
# 🚀 IT Club & Innovation Hub

> A polished, fully-interactive **frontend prototype** for a College IT Club platform — built to demonstrate a proposed digital hub where students can submit ideas, discover projects, find teammates, attend events, take quizzes, and get mentored by faculty. Exceptional projects may be referred to the **Crieya Incubation Center**.

---

## 📸 Demo Roles

The platform supports **three role-based demo views** accessible via the **Demo Mode** switcher:

| Role | Entry Point | Key Features |
|------|-------------|--------------|
| 👨‍🎓 **Student** | `/student/dashboard` | Submit ideas, join projects, find teammates, take quizzes, project chat |
| 👩‍🏫 **Faculty** | `/faculty/dashboard` | Review & approve ideas, mentor projects, schedule sessions |
| 🛡️ **Admin** | `/admin/dashboard` | Platform analytics, manage users/projects/events, post announcements |

---

## ✨ Features

### 🌐 Public Pages
- **Home** — Hero section, platform overview, feature highlights
- **Projects** — Browse & filter all student projects
- **Project Detail** — Full view with tech stack, milestones, team, and join application
- **Events** — Hackathons, workshops, talks with full detail & registration modal
- **Learn** — Articles & quizzes with full interactive quiz player
- **Ideas Board** — Community idea voting + submit your own
- **Workflow** — 10-step visual idea-to-incubation timeline
- **Incubation** — Crieya Incubation Center showcase

### 👨‍🎓 Student Portal
- Dashboard with live stats (projects, applications, events, tasks)
- My Projects, My Ideas with faculty feedback display
- Application tracker with status history
- **Find Teammates** — Browse students by skill and connect
- **Project Chat** — Group messaging demo for project teams
- Learn Hub with quiz retry & score history
- Event registration with capacity tracking
- Mentorship session schedule

### 👩‍🏫 Faculty Portal
- Dashboard with mentored projects and pending idea queue
- **Idea Review** — Approve/reject ideas with reviewer feedback notes
- Mentorship sessions management
- Student progress tracker

### 🛡️ Admin Panel
- Platform analytics with charts (Recharts)
- User, project, event management
- **Announcements** — Broadcast to students, faculty, or all
- Ideas moderation queue

### 🔧 Technical Highlights
- **Global reactive store** (`useAppStore`) persisted to `localStorage` — ideas, applications, notifications, registrations, and quiz results stay in sync across all pages
- Live notification bell with **mark-as-read** and **mark-all-read**
- Multi-step idea submission form with validation and draft saving
- Interactive quiz player with timer, option reveal, explanations, and score ring
- Responsive layouts for all screen sizes

---

## 🛠️ Tech Stack

| Layer | Technology |
|-------|-----------|
| Framework | React 19 + TypeScript |
| Build Tool | Vite 8 |
| Routing | React Router v7 |
| Styling | Tailwind CSS v4 |
| Charts | Recharts |
| Icons | Lucide React |
| State | React Context + `localStorage` |

---

## 📦 Installation & Setup

### Prerequisites

Make sure you have the following installed:

- **Node.js** v18 or higher → [Download](https://nodejs.org/)
- **npm** v9 or higher (comes with Node.js)
- **Git** → [Download](https://git-scm.com/)

Verify your versions:
```bash
node --version   # should be v18+
npm --version    # should be v9+
```

---

### 1. Clone the Repository

```bash
git clone https://github.com/akshatatcodes/it-club.git
cd it-club
```

---

### 2. Install Dependencies

```bash
npm install
```

This will install all required packages listed in `package.json`, including React, Vite, Tailwind, Recharts, and Lucide.

---

### 3. Start the Development Server

```bash
npm run dev
```

The app will start at:

```
http://localhost:5173
```

Open this URL in your browser. The page hot-reloads automatically when you edit files.

---

### 4. Build for Production (Optional)

To create an optimised production build:

```bash
npm run build
```

The output will be in the `dist/` folder. To preview the production build locally:

```bash
npm run preview
```

---

## 🗂️ Project Structure

```
it-club/
├── public/                  # Static assets
├── src/
│   ├── components/
│   │   ├── ui/              # Reusable UI components (Card, Modal, Badge, etc.)
│   │   └── DemoRoleSwitcher.tsx
│   ├── data/
│   │   ├── mock.ts          # All mock data (ideas, events, quizzes, etc.)
│   │   ├── projects.ts      # Project mock data + helpers
│   │   └── users.ts         # User mock data + helpers
│   ├── hooks/
│   │   ├── useAppContext.tsx # DemoRole + Toast context
│   │   └── useAppStore.tsx  # Global reactive demo store
│   ├── layouts/
│   │   ├── PublicLayout.tsx  # Nav + footer for public pages
│   │   └── DashboardLayout.tsx # Sidebar + header for role portals
│   ├── pages/
│   │   ├── public/          # Home, Projects, Events, Learn, Ideas, Workflow, Incubation
│   │   ├── student/         # Student dashboard + all sub-pages
│   │   ├── faculty/         # Faculty dashboard + sub-pages
│   │   └── admin/           # Admin dashboard + sub-pages
│   ├── types/
│   │   └── index.ts         # TypeScript interfaces
│   ├── utils/
│   │   └── index.ts         # Formatters, status configs, helpers
│   ├── App.tsx              # Root router with all routes
│   ├── main.tsx             # React entry point
│   └── index.css            # Global styles + Tailwind + design tokens
├── index.html
├── package.json
├── tsconfig.json
└── vite.config.ts
```

---

## 🎮 How to Navigate the Demo

1. **Open** `http://localhost:5173`
2. **Click "Enter Demo"** on the homepage — or navigate to `/demo`
3. **Select a role** (Student / Faculty / Admin) to enter the respective portal
4. Use the **floating role switcher** (bottom-right corner) to switch roles at any time
5. All interactions (voting, applying, registering, quiz scores, notifications) **persist across page reloads** via `localStorage`

### Key Demo Flows

| Flow | Steps |
|------|-------|
| Submit Idea | Ideas Board → Submit an Idea → Fill multi-step form → Submit |
| Join a Project | Projects → Select project → Request to Join → Fill application |
| Take a Quiz | Learn → Quizzes tab → Start Quiz → Answer questions → View score |
| Review as Faculty | Switch to Faculty → Review Ideas → Approve/Reject with feedback |
| Post Announcement | Switch to Admin → Announcements → New Announcement |

---

## 🔐 Important Notes

> ⚠️ **This is a frontend-only prototype.** There is no backend, database, real authentication, or payment system. All data is mock/static and stored in browser `localStorage`.

- No login credentials are required — just select a role in Demo Mode
- Resetting `localStorage` in browser DevTools will restore all data to defaults
- The platform is designed as a **faculty approval showcase**, not a production application

---

This project is for educational and demonstration purposes only.
